if _G.AmbitiousRunning then return end
_G.AmbitiousRunning = true
if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")

local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")

local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local HubGui = PlayerGui
do
  local ok, res = pcall(function()
    if type(gethui) == "function" then return gethui() end
    if type(get_hidden_gui) == "function" then return get_hidden_gui() end
    return game:GetService("CoreGui")
  end)
  if ok and typeof(res) == "Instance" then HubGui = res end
end
_G.AmbitiousGuiParent = HubGui
local _SV = {} -- setVisual callbacks (merged to save local slots)
function _G._AmbitiousWaitCharReady(character, timeout)
  character = character or LP.Character
  if not character then return false end
  local deadline = os.clock() + (tonumber(timeout) or 5)
  while os.clock() < deadline do
    if not character.Parent then return false end
    if LP.Character and LP.Character ~= character then return false end
    if character:FindFirstChild("Head")
       and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
       and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
      return true
    end
    task.wait(0.05)
  end
  return false
end
_G.AmbitiousIsMobile = true
for _, name in ipairs({"AmbitiousHub"}) do
  local old = HubGui:FindFirstChild(name)
  if old then old:Destroy() end
  old = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild(name)
  if old then old:Destroy() end
end
local NS = 59.5
local CS = 28.8
local LAGGER_SPEED = 29
local LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED = 15
local currentSpeedMode = "Normal"
autoCarrySpeedEnabled = false
setAutoCarrySpeedVisual = nil
_G.AmbitiousAutoCarryWasCarrying = false
_G.AmbitiousAutoCarrySavedMode = nil
_G._AmbitiousAutoCarryMode = _G._AmbitiousAutoCarryMode or "NORMAL"
_G._AmbitiousAutoCarrySoft = _G._AmbitiousAutoCarrySoft or {radius = 9.5, animals = {}, lastScan = 0, scanInterval = 2}
_G._AmbitiousAS = _G._AmbitiousAS or {interval=0, wasLow=false, locked=false, savedMode=nil, restoring=false}
local autoStealEnabled = false
local selectedStealMode = "Normal"
local selectedSemiMode = "V1"
local autoStealRadius = 62
_G.AmbitiousStealRadii = _G.AmbitiousStealRadii or {Normal = 62, Semi = 60, SemiV2Radius = 60, SemiV2SemiRadius = 8}
local autoStealRadiusBox = nil
_G._AmbitiousSyncAfterHit = {
  enabled = false,
  conn = nil,
  setVisual = nil,
  gen = 0,
  wasRagdoll = false,
}
local selectedAimbotMode = "V1"
local AIMBOT_SPEED = 58
local LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED = 40
_G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Speed = _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Speed or 58
if _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2LaggerSpeed == nil or tonumber(_G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2LaggerSpeed) == 58 then _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2LaggerSpeed = 40 end
local autoSwingEnabled = false
local mirrorTPDownEnabled = false
do
  local MAP_BOUNDS = {
    MinX = -535.7,
    MaxX = -422.0,
    MinY = -7.9,
    MaxY = 115.0,
    MinZ = -72.0,
    MaxZ = 193.3,
  }
  local TP_TOTAL   = 150
  local TP_DELTA_Y = 100
  local WINDOW     = 0.07
  local WALK_CLEAR = 15
  local STEP_MAX   = 50   -- oltre questo non e' camminata, non si accumula
  local function enabled() return _G.AmbitiousAntiVoidEnabled == true end
  local ghost   = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })  -- [player] = posizione d'origine
  local ghostCF = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })  -- [player] = CFrame d'origine
  local isOutOf = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })  -- [player] = bool
  local tracked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })  -- [player] = true (thread gia' avviato)
  local function isWithinBounds(pos)
    if typeof(pos) ~= "Vector3" then return false end
    return pos.X >= MAP_BOUNDS.MinX and pos.X <= MAP_BOUNDS.MaxX
       and pos.Y >= MAP_BOUNDS.MinY and pos.Y <= MAP_BOUNDS.MaxY
       and pos.Z >= MAP_BOUNDS.MinZ and pos.Z <= MAP_BOUNDS.MaxZ
  end
  local function onTeleportDetected(player, originCFrame)
    isOutOf[player] = true
    ghostCF[player] = originCFrame
    ghost[player]   = originCFrame.Position
  end
  local _rescueLastSafePos = nil   -- ultima pos a terra dentro la zona
  local _rescueSpawnOrigin = nil   -- prima pos sicura rilevata all'accensione
  local _rescueLastRescue  = 0     -- os.clock() dell'ultimo rescue
  local RESCUE_COOLDOWN    = 0     -- istantaneo (come in Ambitious_2_)
  local RESCUE_UPDATE_INT  = 0.1   -- ogni quanto aggiornare il checkpoint
  local _rescueLastUpdate  = 0
  local function _rescueUpdateCheckpoint()
    local c   = LP.Character
    local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not (hrp and hum) then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - _rescueLastUpdate < RESCUE_UPDATE_INT then return end
    _rescueLastUpdate = now
    local pos = hrp.Position
    if not _rescueSpawnOrigin and isWithinBounds(pos) then
      _rescueSpawnOrigin = pos
    end
    if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air and isWithinBounds(pos) then
      _rescueLastSafePos = pos
    end
  end
  local function _rescueTryRescue()
    local c   = LP.Character
    local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not (hrp and hrp.Parent) then return end
    if isWithinBounds(hrp.Position) then return end
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - _rescueLastRescue < RESCUE_COOLDOWN then return end
    _rescueLastRescue = now
    local dest = _rescueLastSafePos or _rescueSpawnOrigin
    if dest and isWithinBounds(dest) then
      pcall(function()
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity  = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.CFrame = CFrame.new(dest + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
      end)
    end
  end
  RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    if not enabled() then return end
    _rescueUpdateCheckpoint()
    _rescueTryRescue()
  end)
  LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    _rescueLastSafePos = nil
    _rescueSpawnOrigin = nil
    _rescueLastRescue  = 0
    _rescueLastUpdate  = 0
  end)
  local function trackPlayer(player)
    if player == LP then return end
    if tracked[player] then return end
    tracked[player] = true
    local history      = {}
    local walkedXZ     = 0     -- spostamento orizzontale accumulato a piedi
    local lastCheckPos = nil
    task.spawn(function()
      while player and player.Parent do
        if not enabled() then
          isOutOf[player] = false
          history      = {}
          walkedXZ     = 0
          lastCheckPos = nil
          task.wait(0.5)
        else
          local char = player.Character
          local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
          local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
          if hrp and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
            local now = tick()
            local currentCFrame = hrp.CFrame
            local currentPos = currentCFrame.Position
            if isOutOf[player] then
              if lastCheckPos then
                local moveVector = currentPos - lastCheckPos
                local moveXZ = Vector3.new(moveVector.X, 0, moveVector.Z).Magnitude
                if moveXZ < STEP_MAX and isWithinBounds(currentPos) then
                  walkedXZ = walkedXZ + moveXZ
                end
              end
              lastCheckPos = currentPos
              if walkedXZ >= WALK_CLEAR then
                isOutOf[player] = false
                ghost[player]   = currentPos
                ghostCF[player] = currentCFrame
                history      = {}
                walkedXZ     = 0
                lastCheckPos = nil
              end
            else
              walkedXZ     = 0
              lastCheckPos = currentPos
              ghost[player]   = currentPos
              ghostCF[player] = currentCFrame
              table.insert(history, {time = now, cframe = currentCFrame})
              while #history > 0 and (now - history[1].time) > WINDOW do
                table.remove(history, 1)
              end
              local oldest = history[1]
              if oldest then
                local totalDistance = (currentPos - oldest.cframe.Position).Magnitude
                local deltaY = math.abs(currentPos.Y - oldest.cframe.Position.Y)
                if totalDistance >= TP_TOTAL or deltaY >= TP_DELTA_Y then
                  local refCFrame = oldest.cframe
                  local refPos = refCFrame.Position
                  local studsTraveledVector = currentPos - refPos
                  local exactOriginPos = currentPos - studsTraveledVector
                  local exactCFrame = CFrame.new(exactOriginPos) * (refCFrame - refCFrame.Position)
                  if isWithinBounds(exactOriginPos) then
                    onTeleportDetected(player, exactCFrame)
                  end
                  history = {}
                end
              end
            end
          else
            isOutOf[player] = false
            history      = {}
            walkedXZ     = 0
            lastCheckPos = nil
          end
          RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
        end
      end
      tracked[player] = nil
    end)
  end
  Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(trackPlayer)
  local function startAll()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do trackPlayer(plr) end
  end
  local function stopAll()
    for plr in pairs(isOutOf) do isOutOf[plr] = false end
  end
  local function posFor(player, rawPos)
    if not player or not enabled() then return rawPos, false end
    if isOutOf[player] then
      local g = ghost[player]
      if g then return g, true end
    end
    return rawPos, false
  end
  local function posForPart(part, rawPos)
    if not part then return rawPos, false end
    local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
    local player = model and Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model)
    return posFor(player, rawPos or part.Position)
  end
  _G.AmbitiousAntiVoidEnabled = _G.AmbitiousAntiVoidEnabled == true
  startAll()
  _G.AmbitiousAntiVoid = {
    setEnabled = function(on)
      on = on and true or false
      local was = _G.AmbitiousAntiVoidEnabled == true
      _G.AmbitiousAntiVoidEnabled = on
      if on and not was then
        startAll()
      elseif (not on) and was then
        stopAll()
      end
    end,
    isEnabled  = function() return _G.AmbitiousAntiVoidEnabled == true end,
    bounds     = MAP_BOUNDS,
    posFor     = posFor,
    posForPart = posForPart,
    isOut      = function(player) return player and isOutOf[player] == true end,
    ghostOf    = function(player) return player and ghost[player] or nil end,
  }
  local replicas   = {}
  local ghostFolder = nil
  local function ensureFolder()
    if ghostFolder and ghostFolder.Parent then return ghostFolder end
    ghostFolder = Instance.new("Folder")
    ghostFolder.Name = "AmbitiousAntiVoid"
    ghostFolder.Parent = workspace
    return ghostFolder
  end
  local function setRealESP(char, visible)
    if not char then return end
    local hl = char:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubESP")
    if hl then pcall(function() hl.Enabled = visible end) end
    local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local tag = head and head:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubESPTag")
    if tag then pcall(function() tag.Enabled = visible end) end
  end
  local function clearReplica(plr)
    local r = replicas[plr]
    if r then pcall(function() r:Destroy() end) end
    replicas[plr] = nil
  end
  local function buildReplica(plr, char, cf)
    clearReplica(plr)
    if not char:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubESP") then return end
    local ok, clone = pcall(function()
      char.Archivable = true
      return char:Clone()
    end)
    if not ok or not clone then return end
    for _, d in ipairs(clone:GetDescendants()) do
      if d:IsA("Script") or d:IsA("LocalScript") or d:IsA("Humanoid") then
        pcall(function() d:Destroy() end)
      elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
        d.Anchored   = true
        d.CanCollide = false
        d.CanQuery   = false
        d.CanTouch   = false
      end
    end
    clone.Name = "GhostReplica_" .. tostring(plr.Name)
    clone.Parent = ensureFolder()
    pcall(function() clone:PivotTo(cf) end)
    local hl = clone:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubESP")
    if hl then pcall(function() hl.Enabled = true end) end
    local cloneHead = clone:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local tag = cloneHead and cloneHead:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubESPTag")
    if tag then
      pcall(function() tag.Enabled = true end)
      local box = tag:FindFirstChildOfClass("Frame") or tag
      local vl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
      vl.Name = "VoidTag"
      vl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 16)
      vl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 19)
      vl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
      vl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
      vl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
      vl.TextSize = 16
      vl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
      vl.Text = "VOID"
      vl.Parent = box
    end
    replicas[plr] = clone
  end
  local lastMarkerTick = 0
  RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
    local now = os.clock()
    if now - lastMarkerTick < 0.1 then return end
    lastMarkerTick = now
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
      if plr ~= LP then
        local c   = plr.Character
        local hrp = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local out = enabled() and (hrp ~= nil) and (hum ~= nil) and hum.Health > 0 and isOutOf[plr] == true
        if out and ghostCF[plr] then
          setRealESP(c, false)
          if not replicas[plr] or not replicas[plr].Parent then
            buildReplica(plr, c, ghostCF[plr])
          end
        else
          if replicas[plr] then
            clearReplica(plr)
            setRealESP(c, true)
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end)
  Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
    ghost[plr] = nil
    isOutOf[plr] = nil
    ghostCF[plr] = nil
    tracked[plr] = nil
    clearReplica(plr)
  end)
end
_G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotOn = _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotOn or false
_G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2On = _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2On or false
_G._AmbitiousAimbotChase = _G._AmbitiousAimbotChase or { V1 = "NORMAL", V2 = "NORMAL" }
local tpBatAutoSwingEnabled = false
_G.AmbitiousTPBatVersion = _G.AmbitiousTPBatVersion or 1   -- >>> TP BAT VERSIONS <<<
_G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleColor = _G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleColor or Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 230)
_G.AmbitiousTPBatOn = _G.AmbitiousTPBatOn or false
_G.AmbitiousTPBatSilent = false
function _G.AmbitiousTPBatMobileActive()
  if _G.AmbitiousTPBatOn ~= true then return false end
  if _G.AmbitiousTPBatSilent == true then return false end
  if _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2On == true then return false end
  return true
end
local TP_BAT_SPEED = 58
local batCounterEnabled = false
local batCounterMode = "V1"  -- "V1" o "V2"
local medCounterEnabled = false
local antiKickEnabled = false
local espEnabled = false
local showTrackerEnabled = false
local ragdollCountdownEnabled = false
local fpsBoostEnabled = false
local antiLagVisualEnabled = false
local headlessEnabled = false
local korbloxEnabled = false
do
    local VS_FAKE = 16   -- velocita' mostrata al gioco (walkspeed normale)
    local spoofedVelocity = Vector3.zero
    local SPOOF_NAMES = {
        HumanoidRootPart = true,
    }
    local function isSpoofTarget(self)
        if typeof(self) ~= "Instance" then return false end
        if not self:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
        if not SPOOF_NAMES[self.Name] then return false end
        local char = LP.Character
        return char ~= nil and self:IsDescendantOf(char)
    end
    pcall(function()
        if not (getrawmetatable and setreadonly and newcclosure and checkcaller) then
            return
        end
        local mt = getrawmetatable(game)
        setreadonly(mt, false)
        local oldIndex = mt.__index
        mt.__index = newcclosure(function(self, key)
            if not checkcaller() and (key == "AssemblyLinearVelocity" or key == "Velocity") then
                if isSpoofTarget(self) then
                    return spoofedVelocity
                end
            end
            return oldIndex(self, key)
        end)
        local oldNewIndex = mt.__newindex
        mt.__newindex = newcclosure(function(self, key, value)
            if not checkcaller() and (key == "AssemblyLinearVelocity" or key == "Velocity") then
                if isSpoofTarget(self) then
                    spoofedVelocity = value   -- memorizza il valore che l'hub vorrebbe scrivere
                    return                     -- blocca la scrittura reale
                end
            end
            return oldNewIndex(self, key, value)
        end)
        setreadonly(mt, true)
    end)
    local function updateFakeVelocity()
        local char = LP.Character
        local hum  = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if not char or not hum or not root or hum.Health <= 0 then return end
        local dir = hum.MoveDirection
        if dir.Magnitude > 0.05 then
            pcall(function()
                if root.SetNetworkOwner then root:SetNetworkOwner(LP) end
            end)
            local unit = dir.Unit
            spoofedVelocity = Vector3.new(unit.X * VS_FAKE, root.Velocity.Y, unit.Z * VS_FAKE)
        else
            spoofedVelocity = Vector3.new(0, root.Velocity.Y, 0)
        end
    end
    local sig = RunService.PreSimulation or RunService.Stepped
    sig:Connect(updateFakeVelocity)
end
do
  local AntiBatConn = nil
  local Speed     = 10000
  local Angle     = 0
  local Direction = 1   -- 1 aumenta, -1 diminuisce
  local function stopAntiBat()
    if AntiBatConn then
      pcall(function() AntiBatConn:Disconnect() end)
      AntiBatConn = nil
    end
    Speed     = 10000
    Angle     = 0
    Direction = 1
    _G._AmbitiousAntiBatDesiredXZ = nil
    _G._AmbitiousAntiBatSpiking = false
  end
  local function startAntiBat()
    stopAntiBat()
    AntiBatConn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
      if not _G.AmbitiousAntiBatEnabled then return end
      local char = LP.Character
      local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
      if not root or not root.Parent then return end
      local origXZ = Vector3.new(root.Velocity.X, 0, root.Velocity.Z)
      Speed = Speed + (50 * Direction)
      if Speed >= 20000 then
        Speed = 20000
        Direction = -1
      elseif Speed <= 10000 then
        Speed = 10000
        Direction = 1
      end
      Angle = Angle + (math.random() * 0.5)
      local radius = Speed + math.random(-5000, 5000)
      local newX = math.cos(Angle) * radius
      local newZ = math.sin(Angle) * radius
      _G._AmbitiousAntiBatSpiking = true
      root.Velocity = Vector3.new(newX, root.Velocity.Y, newZ)
      RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
      if root.Parent then
        local want = _G._AmbitiousAntiBatDesiredXZ
        if want then
          root.Velocity = Vector3.new(want.X, root.Velocity.Y, want.Z)
        else
          root.Velocity = Vector3.new(origXZ.X, root.Velocity.Y, origXZ.Z)
        end
      end
      _G._AmbitiousAntiBatSpiking = false
    end)
  end
  _G.AmbitiousAntiBatEnabled = _G.AmbitiousAntiBatEnabled == true
  local function step(_, fn)
    pcall(fn)
  end
  _G.AmbitiousAntiBat = {
    setEnabled = function(on)
      on = on and true or false
      _G.AmbitiousAntiBatEnabled = on
      if on then
        step("startAntiBat", startAntiBat)
      else
        step("stopAntiBat", stopAntiBat)
      end
    end,
    isEnabled = function() return _G.AmbitiousAntiBatEnabled == true end,
  }
  task.spawn(function()
    while true do
      task.wait(0.5)
      if _G.AmbitiousAntiBatEnabled then
        if not AntiBatConn then
          step("watchdog:start", startAntiBat)
        end
      elseif AntiBatConn then
        step("watchdog:stop", stopAntiBat)
      end
    end
  end)
  LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    if _G._AmbitiousWaitCharReady then _G._AmbitiousWaitCharReady(char, 10) end
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiBatEnabled then
      startAntiBat()
    else
      stopAntiBat()
    end
  end)
end
;(function()
  local ATP_active      = false
  local voidPart        = nil
  local jitterThread    = nil
  local jitterRunning   = false
  local spamThread      = nil
  local lockConn        = nil
  local frozenCFrame    = nil
  local savedWalkSpeed  = nil
  local savedJumpPower  = nil
  local savedAutoRotate = nil
  _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled = _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled == true
  _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam    = _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam == true
  _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion) or 1, 1, 4)
  local function getHRP()
    local c = LP.Character
    if not c then return nil, nil end
    return c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"), c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
  end
  local function setPartAppearance(part, version)
    local color, transparency
    if version == 1 then
      color, transparency = Color3.fromRGB(255, 50, 50), 0.5
    elseif version == 2 then
      color, transparency = Color3.fromRGB(50, 100, 255), 0.5
    elseif version == 3 then
      color, transparency = Color3.fromRGB(50, 255, 50), 0.5
    elseif version == 4 then
      color, transparency = Color3.fromRGB(200, 50, 200), 0.5
    else
      color, transparency = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1
    end
    pcall(function()
      part.Color = color
      part.Transparency = transparency
    end)
  end
  local function repTo(target)
    local hrp = getHRP()
    if hrp and sethiddenproperty and target then
      pcall(function() sethiddenproperty(hrp, "PhysicsRepRootPart", target) end)
    end
  end
  local function destroyVoidPart()
    if voidPart then
      local hrp = getHRP()
      if hrp then repTo(hrp) end
      pcall(function() voidPart:Destroy() end)
      voidPart = nil
    end
  end
  local function makePart(ver, pos)
    local p = Instance.new("Part")
    p.Name       = "DesyncTrapPart"
    p.Size       = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
    p.CanCollide = false
    p.Anchored   = true
    p.Position   = pos
    setPartAppearance(p, ver)
    p.Parent = workspace
    return p
  end
  local function createVoidPart()
    if voidPart then
      pcall(function() voidPart:Destroy() end)
      voidPart = nil
    end
    local ver = _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion or 1
    local pos
    if ver == 4 then
      pos = Vector3.new(400, 0, 400)
    elseif ver == 3 then
      local rx, rz = math.random(-300, 300), math.random(-300, 300)
      if rx == 0 and rz == 0 then rx, rz = 1, 1 end
      pos = Vector3.new(rx, 0, rz)
    elseif ver == 2 then
      pos = Vector3.new(0, -120, 0)
    else
      pos = Vector3.new(99999999999, 99999999999, 99999999999)
    end
    voidPart = makePart(ver, pos)
  end
  local function stopVoidJitter()
    jitterRunning = false
    if jitterThread then
      pcall(task.cancel, jitterThread)
      jitterThread = nil
    end
  end
  local function startVoidJitter()
    stopVoidJitter()
    jitterRunning = true
    jitterThread = task.spawn(function()
      while jitterRunning and ATP_active and _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion == 3 do
        local rx, rz
        repeat
          rx = math.random(-300, 300)
          rz = math.random(-300, 300)
        until not (rx == 0 and rz == 0)
        if voidPart then
          pcall(function() voidPart:Destroy() end)
          voidPart = nil
        end
        voidPart = makePart(3, Vector3.new(rx, 0, rz))
        repTo(voidPart)
        task.wait(0)
      end
    end)
  end
  local FLOAT = {
    RISE    = 17,     -- di quanto salire rispetto alla Y di partenza
    RATE    = 55,     -- forza di salita, studs al secondo
    CYCLE   = 2.5,    -- quanto si resta su prima di scendere
    PAUSE   = 0.5,    -- pausa a terra prima di risalire
    conn    = nil,
    thread  = nil,
    running = false,
    locked  = false,  -- true da quando la quota e' stata raggiunta
    targetY = nil,    -- quota di questo giro, calcolata alla partenza
  }
  function FLOAT.startLoop()
    if FLOAT.conn then return end
    FLOAT.locked = false
    FLOAT.targetY = nil
    local _, hum0 = getHRP()
    if hum0 then
      pcall(function() hum0:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall) end)
    end
    FLOAT.conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
      local root = getHRP()
      if not root then return end            -- morto o in respawn
      local pos = root.Position
      local vel = root.Velocity
      local rot = root.CFrame - root.CFrame.Position
      if not FLOAT.targetY then
        FLOAT.targetY = pos.Y + FLOAT.RISE
      end
      if not FLOAT.locked then
        local nextY = math.min(pos.Y + FLOAT.RATE * dt, FLOAT.targetY)
        root.CFrame   = CFrame.new(pos.X, nextY, pos.Z) * rot
        root.Velocity = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
        if nextY >= FLOAT.targetY then FLOAT.locked = true end
        return
      end
      root.CFrame   = CFrame.new(pos.X, FLOAT.targetY, pos.Z) * rot
      root.Velocity = Vector3.new(vel.X, 0, vel.Z)
    end)
  end
  function FLOAT.stopLoop(tpDown)
    if FLOAT.conn then
      pcall(function() FLOAT.conn:Disconnect() end)
      FLOAT.conn = nil
    end
    if tpDown and _G.AmbitiousRunTPDown then
      pcall(_G.AmbitiousRunTPDown, true)
    end
  end
  function FLOAT.waitGrounded(timeout)
    local deadline = os.clock() + (timeout or 3)
    while os.clock() < deadline do
      if not FLOAT.running then return end
      local hrp, hum = getHRP()
      if not hrp or not hum then return end
      if hum.Health <= 0 then return end
      if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
        task.wait(FLOAT.PAUSE)   -- pausa a terra, poi si risale
        return
      end
      RunService.Heartbeat:Wait()
    end
  end
  function FLOAT.enable()
    if FLOAT.running then return end
    FLOAT.running = true
    FLOAT.startLoop()
    FLOAT.thread = task.spawn(function()
      while FLOAT.running do
        local t0 = os.clock()
        while FLOAT.running and (os.clock() - t0) < FLOAT.CYCLE do
          task.wait(0.1)
        end
        if not FLOAT.running then break end
        FLOAT.stopLoop(true)
        FLOAT.waitGrounded(3)
        if not FLOAT.running then break end
        FLOAT.startLoop()
      end
    end)
  end
  function FLOAT.disable()
    if not FLOAT.running then return end
    FLOAT.running = false
    if FLOAT.thread then
      pcall(task.cancel, FLOAT.thread)
      FLOAT.thread = nil
    end
    FLOAT.stopLoop(true)
  end
  _G.AmbitiousFloatOn = false
  local function floatSync()
    _G.AmbitiousFloatOn = FLOAT.running == true
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshMobileButtonStates then
      pcall(_G.AmbitiousRefreshMobileButtonStates)
    end
  end
  function _G.AmbitiousFloatStart()
    if FLOAT.running then return end
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiDieArmNow then pcall(_G.AmbitiousAntiDieArmNow) end
    FLOAT.enable()
    floatSync()
  end
  function _G.AmbitiousFloatStop()
    if not FLOAT.running then return end
    FLOAT.disable()
    floatSync()
  end
  function _G.AmbitiousFloatToggle()
    if FLOAT.running then _G.AmbitiousFloatStop() else _G.AmbitiousFloatStart() end
  end
  _G.AmbitiousFloat = {
    start     = function() _G.AmbitiousFloatStart() end,
    stop      = function() _G.AmbitiousFloatStop() end,
    toggle    = function() _G.AmbitiousFloatToggle() end,
    isEnabled = function() return FLOAT.running == true end,
  }
  local function lockMovement()
    local hrp, hum = getHRP()
    if not hrp then return end
    frozenCFrame = hrp.CFrame
    if hum then
      savedWalkSpeed  = hum.WalkSpeed
      savedJumpPower  = hum.JumpPower
      savedAutoRotate = hum.AutoRotate
      pcall(function()
        hum.WalkSpeed  = 0
        hum.JumpPower  = 0
        hum.AutoRotate = false
      end)
    end
    if lockConn then pcall(function() lockConn:Disconnect() end) end
    lockConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
      local r = getHRP()
      if frozenCFrame and r and r.Parent then
        r.CFrame = frozenCFrame
      end
    end)
  end
  local function unlockMovement()
    local _, hum = getHRP()
    if hum then
      pcall(function()
        hum.WalkSpeed  = savedWalkSpeed or 16
        hum.JumpPower  = savedJumpPower or 50
        if savedAutoRotate == nil then
          hum.AutoRotate = true
        else
          hum.AutoRotate = savedAutoRotate
        end
      end)
    end
    savedWalkSpeed, savedJumpPower, savedAutoRotate = nil, nil, nil
    if lockConn then
      pcall(function() lockConn:Disconnect() end)
      lockConn = nil
    end
    frozenCFrame = nil
  end
  local function enableCore()
    if ATP_active then return end
    ATP_active = true
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiDieArmNow then pcall(_G.AmbitiousAntiDieArmNow) end
    createVoidPart()
    repTo(voidPart)
    lockMovement()
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion == 3 then startVoidJitter() end
  end
  local function disableCore()
    if not ATP_active then return end
    ATP_active = false
    stopVoidJitter()
    unlockMovement()
    destroyVoidPart()
  end
  local function applyDesync()
    if not ATP_active then return end
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion == 3 then return end
    if not voidPart or not voidPart.Parent then createVoidPart() end
    repTo(voidPart)
  end
  RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(applyDesync)
  RunService.Stepped:Connect(applyDesync)
  local function stopSpamLoop()
    if spamThread then
      pcall(task.cancel, spamThread)
      spamThread = nil
    end
  end
  local function startSpamLoop()
    stopSpamLoop()
    spamThread = task.spawn(function()
      enableCore()
      while _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam and _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled do
        task.wait(0.5)
        if not (_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam and _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled) then break end
        disableCore()
        task.wait(0.5)
        if not (_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam and _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled) then break end
        enableCore()
      end
      if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled then enableCore() else disableCore() end
    end)
  end
  local function refreshUI()
    pcall(function()
      if _G._AmbitiousAntiTPBatSetRowVisual then
        _G._AmbitiousAntiTPBatSetRowVisual(_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled == true)
      end
      if _G._AmbitiousAntiTPBatSetSpamVisual then
        _G._AmbitiousAntiTPBatSetSpamVisual(_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam == true)
      end
      if _G._AmbitiousAntiTPBatSetVersionVisual then
        _G._AmbitiousAntiTPBatSetVersionVisual(_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion or 1)
      end
      if _G.AmbitiousRefreshMobileButtonStates then
        _G.AmbitiousRefreshMobileButtonStates()
      end
      if _G.AmbitiousAntiDieAutoSync then _G.AmbitiousAntiDieAutoSync() end
    end)
  end
  _G._AmbitiousAntiTPBatRefreshUI = refreshUI
  local function saveCfg()
    if type(saveAmbitiousConfig) == "function" then pcall(saveAmbitiousConfig) end
  end
  local function setEnabled(on, skipSave)
    on = on and true or false
    _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled = on
    if on then
      if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam then
        startSpamLoop()
      else
        stopSpamLoop()
        enableCore()
      end
    else
      stopSpamLoop()
      disableCore()
    end
    refreshUI()
    if not skipSave then saveCfg() end
  end
  local function setSpam(on, skipSave)
    on = on and true or false
    _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam = on
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled then
      if on then
        startSpamLoop()
      else
        stopSpamLoop()
        enableCore()
      end
    else
      stopSpamLoop()
    end
    refreshUI()
    if not skipSave then saveCfg() end
  end
  local function setVersion(v, skipSave)
    v = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 1, 1, 4)
    local changed = (v ~= _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion)
    if changed then
      stopSpamLoop()
      disableCore()
      _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion = v
      if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled then
        if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam then startSpamLoop() else enableCore() end
      end
    end
    refreshUI()
    if changed and not skipSave then saveCfg() end
  end
  local function hardStop()
    if not (ATP_active or _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled) then return end
    _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled = false
    stopSpamLoop()
    disableCore()
    refreshUI()
    saveCfg()
  end
  local function onDeadTrigger()
    if ATP_active or _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled then hardStop() end
  end
  local enemyListeners = {}
  local function clearEnemyListeners(plr)
    local t = enemyListeners[plr]
    if t then
      for _, c in ipairs(t) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
      enemyListeners[plr] = nil
    end
  end
  local function watchEnemyCharacter(plr, character)
    clearEnemyListeners(plr)
    if not character then return end
    local conns = {}
    local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
      table.insert(conns, hum.Died:Connect(onDeadTrigger))
      table.insert(conns, hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(hp)
        if hp <= 0 then onDeadTrigger() end
      end))
    end
    table.insert(conns, character.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
      if not parent then onDeadTrigger() end
    end))
    enemyListeners[plr] = conns
  end
  local function setupEnemyPlayer(plr)
    if plr == LP then return end
    if plr.Character then watchEnemyCharacter(plr, plr.Character) end
    plr.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c) watchEnemyCharacter(plr, c) end)
  end
  for _, p in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do pcall(setupEnemyPlayer, p) end
  Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(p) pcall(setupEnemyPlayer, p) end)
  Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p) pcall(clearEnemyListeners, p) end)
  local selfConns = {}
  local wasRagdolled = false
  local function clearSelfConns()
    for _, c in ipairs(selfConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
    selfConns = {}
  end
  local function setupSelfWatchers(character)
    clearSelfConns()
    wasRagdolled = false
    if not character then return end
    local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
      table.insert(selfConns, hum.Died:Connect(onDeadTrigger))
      table.insert(selfConns, hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(hp)
        if hp <= 0 then onDeadTrigger() end
      end))
      table.insert(selfConns, hum.StateChanged:Connect(function(_, newState)
        local isRag = (newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics)
        pcall(function()
          if newState == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll then isRag = true end
        end)
        if isRag and not wasRagdolled then
          wasRagdolled = true
          onDeadTrigger()
        elseif not isRag then
          wasRagdolled = false
        end
      end))
    end
    table.insert(selfConns, character.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
      if not parent then onDeadTrigger() end
    end))
  end
  if LP.Character then setupSelfWatchers(LP.Character) end
  LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
    if _G._AmbitiousWaitCharReady then pcall(_G._AmbitiousWaitCharReady, c, 10) end
    setupSelfWatchers(c)
    _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled = false
    stopSpamLoop()
    disableCore()
    refreshUI()
  end)
  _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBat = {
    setEnabled = function(on, skipSave) setEnabled(on, skipSave) end,
    isEnabled  = function() return _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled == true end,
    toggle     = function() setEnabled(not (_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled == true)) end,
    setSpam    = function(on, skipSave) setSpam(on, skipSave) end,
    getSpam    = function() return _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam == true end,
    setVersion = function(v, skipSave) setVersion(v, skipSave) end,
    getVersion = function() return _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion or 1 end,
    hardStop   = hardStop,
  }
  _G.AmbitiousToggleAntiTPBat = function()
    setEnabled(not (_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled == true))
  end
  task.spawn(function()
    while true do
      task.wait(0.5)
      if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled then
        if not _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam and not ATP_active then
          pcall(enableCore)
        end
      elseif ATP_active then
        pcall(disableCore)
      end
    end
  end)
end)()
do
    local _Players    = game:GetService("Players")
    local _RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
    local _LP         = _Players.LocalPlayer
    local CT_ITEMS = {
        { key = "Bat",    match = function(n) n = n:lower() return n:find("bat") ~= nil or n:find("slap") ~= nil end },
        { key = "Medusa", match = function(n) return n:lower():find("medusa") ~= nil end },
    }
    local function ctMatchItem(inst)
        if not (inst:IsA("Tool") or inst:IsA("Model") or inst:IsA("Accessory") or inst:IsA("BasePart")) then
            return nil
        end
        for _, item in ipairs(CT_ITEMS) do
            if item.match(inst.Name) then return item end
        end
        return nil
    end
    local function isReplicaStuff(d)
        if d.Name == "LocalReplica" then return true end
        return d:FindFirstAncestor("LocalReplica") ~= nil
    end
    local CT_Trans = {}
    do
        local PRIORITY = { "custom", "rainbow", "transparent" }
        local baseline = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local claims   = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local guards   = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        function CT_Trans.getBaseline(part)
            local b = baseline[part]
            if b ~= nil then return b end
            local ok, val = pcall(function() return part.Transparency end)
            if not ok then return nil end
            baseline[part] = val
            return val
        end
        local function wantedValue(part)
            local c = claims[part]
            if not c then return nil end
            for _, who in ipairs(PRIORITY) do
                if c[who] ~= nil then return c[who] end
            end
            return nil
        end
        local function setIfDifferent(part, v)
            local ok, cur = pcall(function() return part.Transparency end)
            if ok and cur ~= nil and math.abs(cur - v) <= 0.001 then return end
            pcall(function() part.Transparency = v end)
        end
        local function resolve(part)
            local v = wantedValue(part)
            if v ~= nil then
                setIfDifferent(part, v)
                return
            end
            local b = baseline[part]
            if b ~= nil then
                setIfDifferent(part, b)
            end
        end
        local function armGuard(part)
            if guards[part] then return end
            local ok, conn = pcall(function()
                return part:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Transparency"):Connect(function()
                    local v = wantedValue(part)
                    if v == nil then return end
                    local ok2, cur = pcall(function() return part.Transparency end)
                    if ok2 and cur ~= nil and math.abs(cur - v) > 0.001 then
                        pcall(function() part.Transparency = v end)
                    end
                end)
            end)
            if ok and conn then guards[part] = conn end
        end
        local function disarmGuard(part)
            local conn = guards[part]
            if conn then
                pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end)
                guards[part] = nil
            end
        end
        function CT_Trans.enforce()
            for part, _ in pairs(claims) do
                if part.Parent then
                    local v = wantedValue(part)
                    if v ~= nil then
                        local ok, cur = pcall(function() return part.Transparency end)
                        if ok and cur ~= nil and math.abs(cur - v) > 0.001 then
                            pcall(function() part.Transparency = v end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        function CT_Trans.claim(part, who, value)
            if not part then return end
            if CT_Trans.getBaseline(part) == nil then return end
            local c = claims[part]
            if not c then c = {}; claims[part] = c end
            c[who] = value
            armGuard(part)
            resolve(part)
        end
        function CT_Trans.release(part, who)
            if not part then return end
            local c = claims[part]
            if not c then return end
            c[who] = nil
            if next(c) == nil then
                claims[part] = nil
                disarmGuard(part)
            end
            if part.Parent then resolve(part) end
        end
    end
    local CT_JANITORS = {}
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(10)
            for _, fn in ipairs(CT_JANITORS) do pcall(fn) end
        end
    end)
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            task.wait(0.25)
            if _G.AmbitiousCustomToolsEnabled == true
            or _G.AmbitiousTransparentToolsEnabled == true
            or _G.AmbitiousRainbowToolsEnabled == true then
                pcall(CT_Trans.enforce)
            end
        end
    end)
    do
        local SPEED       = 0.35
        local SPREAD      = 0.035
        local UPDATE_STEP = 1 / 40
        local enabled  = false
        local tracked  = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local watchers = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local unregister
        local function classify(d)
            if d:IsA("MeshPart") then
                return { obj = d, kind = "part", props = { "Color", "Material", "TextureID" }, clearTexture = "TextureID" }
            elseif d:IsA("BasePart") then
                return { obj = d, kind = "part", props = { "Color", "Material" } }
            elseif d:IsA("SurfaceAppearance") then
                return { obj = d, kind = "surface", props = { "Parent" } }
            elseif d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
                return { obj = d, kind = "decal", props = {} }
            elseif d:IsA("SpecialMesh") then
                return { obj = d, kind = "vector", prop = "VertexColor", props = { "VertexColor", "TextureId" }, clearTexture = "TextureId" }
            elseif d:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or d:IsA("Beam") or d:IsA("Trail") then
                return { obj = d, kind = "sequence", prop = "Color", props = { "Color" } }
            elseif d:IsA("PointLight") or d:IsA("SpotLight") or d:IsA("SurfaceLight") then
                return { obj = d, kind = "color", prop = "Color", props = { "Color" } }
            elseif d:IsA("Highlight") then
                return { obj = d, kind = "color", prop = "FillColor", props = { "FillColor" } }
            end
            return nil
        end
        local function snapshot(node)
            node.original = {}
            for _, prop in ipairs(node.props) do
                local ok, val = pcall(function() return node.obj[prop] end)
                if ok then node.original[prop] = val end
            end
        end
        local function restoreNode(node)
            if node.kind == "decal" then CT_Trans.release(node.obj, "rainbow") end
            if not node.original then return end
            for prop, val in pairs(node.original) do
                pcall(function() node.obj[prop] = val end)
            end
        end
        local function applyOnce(node)
            if node.kind == "surface" then
                pcall(function() node.obj.Parent = nil end)
            elseif node.kind == "decal" then
                CT_Trans.claim(node.obj, "rainbow", 1)
            elseif node.clearTexture then
                pcall(function() node.obj[node.clearTexture] = "" end)
            end
        end
        local function addNode(entry, d)
            local node = classify(d)
            if not node then return end
            snapshot(node)
            applyOnce(node)
            if node.kind == "part" then
                table.insert(entry.parts, node)
            elseif node.kind == "surface" or node.kind == "decal" then
                table.insert(entry.statics, node)
            else
                table.insert(entry.effects, node)
            end
        end
        local function register(root)
            if tracked[root] then return end
            local entry = { parts = {}, effects = {}, statics = {}, conns = {} }
            tracked[root] = entry
            for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do addNode(entry, d) end
            if root:IsA("BasePart") then addNode(entry, root) end
            table.insert(entry.conns, root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                addNode(entry, d)
            end))
            table.insert(entry.conns, root.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if not parent then unregister(root, false) end
            end))
        end
        function unregister(root, doRestore)
            local entry = tracked[root]
            if not entry then return end
            for _, conn in ipairs(entry.conns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
            if doRestore then
                for _, node in ipairs(entry.parts)   do restoreNode(node) end
                for _, node in ipairs(entry.effects) do restoreNode(node) end
                for _, node in ipairs(entry.statics) do restoreNode(node) end
            end
            tracked[root] = nil
        end
        local function tryRegister(inst)
            if not enabled then return end
            if ctMatchItem(inst) then register(inst) end
        end
        local function unwatch(container)
            local conns = watchers[container]
            if not conns then return end
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            watchers[container] = nil
        end
        local function watch(container)
            if not container or watchers[container] then return end
            watchers[container] = { container.ChildAdded:Connect(tryRegister) }
            for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do tryRegister(child) end
        end
        local function watchAll()
            watch(_LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
            watch(_LP.Character)
        end
        _LP.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if enabled and child:IsA("Backpack") then watch(child) end
        end)
        _LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            for root in pairs(tracked) do unregister(root, false) end
            for container in pairs(watchers) do unwatch(container) end
            if not enabled then return end
            watch(char)
            task.defer(function()
                if enabled then watchAll() end
            end)
        end)
        local acc = 0
        _RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
            if not enabled then return end
            if next(tracked) == nil then return end
            acc = acc + dt
            if acc < UPDATE_STEP then return end
            acc = 0
            local baseHue = (tick() * SPEED) % 1
            for _, entry in pairs(tracked) do
                for i, node in ipairs(entry.parts) do
                    local part = node.obj
                    if part.Parent then
                        pcall(function()
                            part.Color = Color3.fromHSV((baseHue + (i - 1) * SPREAD) % 1, 1, 1)
                        end)
                    end
                end
                for i, node in ipairs(entry.effects) do
                    if node.obj.Parent then
                        local col = Color3.fromHSV((baseHue + (i - 1) * SPREAD) % 1, 1, 1)
                        if node.kind == "sequence" then
                            pcall(function() node.obj[node.prop] = ColorSequence.new(col) end)
                        elseif node.kind == "vector" then
                            pcall(function() node.obj[node.prop] = Vector3.new(col.R, col.G, col.B) end)
                        else
                            pcall(function() node.obj[node.prop] = col end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
        table.insert(CT_JANITORS, function()
            for root in pairs(tracked) do
                if not root.Parent then unregister(root, false) end
            end
            for container in pairs(watchers) do
                if container ~= workspace and not container.Parent then unwatch(container) end
            end
        end)
        _G.AmbitiousRainbowTools = {
            setEnabled = function(on)
                on = on and true or false
                enabled = on
                _G.AmbitiousRainbowToolsEnabled = on
                if on then
                    watchAll()
                else
                    for root in pairs(tracked) do unregister(root, true) end
                    for container in pairs(watchers) do unwatch(container) end
                end
            end,
            isEnabled = function() return enabled end,
        }
    end
    do
        local TRANSPARENCY = 0.5   -- 0 = opaco, 1 = invisibile
        local enabled  = false
        local tracked  = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local watchers = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local unregister
        local function canTouch(d)
            if not d:IsA("BasePart") then return false end
            local base = CT_Trans.getBaseline(d)
            if base == nil or base >= 1 then return false end
            return true
        end
        local function addNode(entry, d)
            if not canTouch(d) then return end
            if entry.seen[d] then return end
            entry.seen[d] = true
            local node = { obj = d }
            table.insert(entry.parts, node)
            CT_Trans.claim(d, "transparent", TRANSPARENCY)
        end
        local function register(root)
            if tracked[root] then return end
            local entry = { parts = {}, seen = {}, conns = {} }
            tracked[root] = entry
            for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do addNode(entry, d) end
            if root:IsA("BasePart") then addNode(entry, root) end
            table.insert(entry.conns, root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                addNode(entry, d)
            end))
            table.insert(entry.conns, root.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if not parent then unregister(root, false) end
            end))
        end
        function unregister(root, doRestore)
            local entry = tracked[root]
            if not entry then return end
            for _, conn in ipairs(entry.conns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
            for _, node in ipairs(entry.parts) do
                CT_Trans.release(node.obj, "transparent")
            end
            tracked[root] = nil
        end
        local function tryRegister(inst)
            if not enabled then return end
            if ctMatchItem(inst) then register(inst) end
        end
        local function unwatch(container)
            local conns = watchers[container]
            if not conns then return end
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            watchers[container] = nil
        end
        local function watch(container)
            if not container or watchers[container] then return end
            watchers[container] = { container.ChildAdded:Connect(tryRegister) }
            for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do tryRegister(child) end
        end
        local function watchAll()
            watch(_LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
            watch(_LP.Character)
        end
        _LP.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if enabled and child:IsA("Backpack") then watch(child) end
        end)
        _LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            for root in pairs(tracked) do unregister(root, false) end
            for container in pairs(watchers) do unwatch(container) end
            if not enabled then return end
            watch(char)
            task.defer(function()
                if enabled then watchAll() end
            end)
        end)
        table.insert(CT_JANITORS, function()
            for root in pairs(tracked) do
                if not root.Parent then unregister(root, false) end
            end
            for container in pairs(watchers) do
                if not container.Parent then unwatch(container) end
            end
        end)
        _G.AmbitiousTransparentTools = {
            setEnabled = function(on)
                on = on and true or false
                enabled = on
                _G.AmbitiousTransparentToolsEnabled = on
                if on then
                    watchAll()
                else
                    for root in pairs(tracked) do unregister(root, true) end
                    for container in pairs(watchers) do unwatch(container) end
                end
            end,
            isEnabled = function() return enabled end,
        }
    end
    do
        local ASSETS = {
            DiamondSword      = { cat = "Bat",    label = "Diamond Sword",
                                  mesh = "rbxassetid://8827558932", tex = "rbxassetid://8827558969",
                                  scale = Vector3.new(0.2, 0.2, 0.2),
                                  c0 = CFrame.new(-0.05, -0.1, -0.12) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), math.rad(180), 300),
                                  view = 4.9 },
            Katana            = { cat = "Bat",    label = "Katana",
                                  mesh = "rbxassetid://13528902482", tex = "rbxassetid://13528902373",
                                  scale = Vector3.new(1.4, 1.4, 1.4),
                                  c0 = CFrame.new(0, 0.6, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(270), math.rad(180), math.rad(180)),
                                  view = 6.6 },
            Skull             = { cat = "Medusa", label = "Skull",
                                  mesh = "rbxassetid://2050312704", tex = "rbxassetid://2050313393",
                                  scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1),
                                  c0 = CFrame.new(0, 0.65, -0.4) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(330), 0, 0),
                                  view = 4.2 },
            GoldenDesertEagle = { cat = "Medusa", label = "Golden Desert Eagle",
                                  mesh = "rbxassetid://430251413", tex = "rbxassetid://435840335",
                                  scale = Vector3.new(0.01, 0.01, 0.01),
                                  c0 = CFrame.new(0, 0, -0.8) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(330), math.rad(180), 0),
                                  view = 3.0 },
        }
        _G.AmbitiousCustomToolAssets = ASSETS
        _G.AmbitiousCustomToolSkins = _G.AmbitiousCustomToolSkins
            or { Bat = "DiamondSword", Medusa = "GoldenDesertEagle" }
        local function skinFor(cat)
            local key = _G.AmbitiousCustomToolSkins[cat]
            if key and ASSETS[key] and ASSETS[key].cat == cat then return key end
            return (cat == "Bat") and "DiamondSword" or "GoldenDesertEagle"
        end
        local enabled       = false
        local tracked       = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })  -- [tool] = { hidden, conns }
        local watchers      = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local pendingHandle = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })  -- [tool] = conn
        local teardown
        local function buildReplica(handle, mode)
            local existing = handle:FindFirstChild("LocalReplica")
            if existing then existing:Destroy() end
            local data = ASSETS[mode] or ASSETS.DiamondSword
            local m = Instance.new("Model")
            m.Name = "LocalReplica"
            local p = Instance.new("Part")
            p.Name        = "MainPart"
            p.CanCollide  = false
            p.CanQuery    = false
            p.CanTouch    = false
            p.Massless    = true
            p.Size        = Vector3.new(1, 1, 1)
            p.Transparency= 0
            p.Anchored    = false
            p.Parent      = m
            local mesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
            mesh.MeshType  = Enum.MeshType.FileMesh
            mesh.MeshId    = data.mesh
            mesh.TextureId = data.tex
            mesh.Parent    = p
            local w = Instance.new("Motor6D")
            w.Part0 = handle
            w.Part1 = p
            mesh.Scale = data.scale
            w.C0 = data.c0
            w.Parent = p
            m.Parent = handle
            return m
        end
        local function hideNode(entry, d)
            if isReplicaStuff(d) then return end
            if d:IsA("BasePart") or d:IsA("Decal") or d:IsA("Texture") then
                entry.hidden[d] = true
                CT_Trans.claim(d, "custom", 1)
            end
        end
        local function setupTool(tool)
            if tracked[tool] then return end
            local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if not handle then
                if pendingHandle[tool] then return end
                local c
                c = tool.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                    if child.Name == "Handle" then
                        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                        pendingHandle[tool] = nil
                        if enabled then setupTool(tool) end
                    end
                end)
                pendingHandle[tool] = c
                return
            end
            local entry = { hidden = {}, conns = {} }
            tracked[tool] = entry
            for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do hideNode(entry, d) end
            local item = ctMatchItem(tool)
            local mode = skinFor((item and item.key == "Bat") and "Bat" or "Medusa")
            entry.cat = (item and item.key == "Bat") and "Bat" or "Medusa"
            buildReplica(handle, mode)
            table.insert(entry.conns, tool.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                task.defer(function()
                    if tracked[tool] then hideNode(entry, d) end
                end)
            end))
            table.insert(entry.conns, tool.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if not parent then teardown(tool, false) end
            end))
        end
        function teardown(tool, doRestore)
            local entry = tracked[tool]
            if not entry then return end
            for _, conn in ipairs(entry.conns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
            local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if handle then
                local rep = handle:FindFirstChild("LocalReplica")
                if rep then pcall(function() rep:Destroy() end) end
            end
            for obj in pairs(entry.hidden) do
                CT_Trans.release(obj, "custom")
            end
            entry.hidden = {}
            tracked[tool] = nil
        end
        local function tryRegister(inst)
            if not enabled then return end
            if inst:IsA("Tool") and ctMatchItem(inst) then
                setupTool(inst)
            end
        end
        local function unwatch(container)
            local conns = watchers[container]
            if not conns then return end
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            watchers[container] = nil
        end
        local function watch(container)
            if not container or watchers[container] then return end
            watchers[container] = { container.ChildAdded:Connect(tryRegister) }
            for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do tryRegister(child) end
        end
        local function watchAll()
            watch(_LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
            watch(_LP.Character)
        end
        _LP.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if enabled and child:IsA("Backpack") then watch(child) end
        end)
        _LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            for tool in pairs(tracked) do teardown(tool, false) end
            for _, c in pairs(pendingHandle) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            table.clear(pendingHandle)
            for container in pairs(watchers) do unwatch(container) end
            if not enabled then return end
            watch(char)
            task.defer(function()
                if enabled then watchAll() end
            end)
        end)
        table.insert(CT_JANITORS, function()
            for tool in pairs(tracked) do
                if not tool.Parent then teardown(tool, false) end
            end
            for tool, c in pairs(pendingHandle) do
                if not tool.Parent then
                    pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                    pendingHandle[tool] = nil
                end
            end
            for container in pairs(watchers) do
                if not container.Parent then unwatch(container) end
            end
        end)
        local function rebuildIfNeeded(tool, entry, deep)
            local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if not handle then return end
            local rep      = handle:FindFirstChild("LocalReplica")
            local mainPart = rep and rep:FindFirstChild("MainPart")
            local motor    = mainPart and mainPart:FindFirstChildOfClass("Motor6D")
            local broken   = (not rep) or (not mainPart) or (not motor) or motor.Part0 ~= handle
            if broken then
                local item = ctMatchItem(tool)
                buildReplica(handle, skinFor((item and item.key == "Bat") and "Bat" or "Medusa"))
            end
            if broken or deep then
                for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do hideNode(entry, d) end
            end
        end
        local function integrityPass(deep)
            if not enabled then return end
            watchAll()
            local bp = _LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
            if bp then for _, ch in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do tryRegister(ch) end end
            local chr = _LP.Character
            if chr then for _, ch in ipairs(chr:GetChildren()) do tryRegister(ch) end end
            for tool, entry in pairs(tracked) do
                if not tool.Parent then
                    teardown(tool, false)
                else
                    rebuildIfNeeded(tool, entry, deep)
                end
            end
        end
        _G.AmbitiousCustomToolsRecheck = function() pcall(integrityPass, true) end
        _G.AmbitiousSetCustomToolSkin = function(cat, key)
            if not ASSETS[key] or ASSETS[key].cat ~= cat then return end
            _G.AmbitiousCustomToolSkins[cat] = key
            for tool, entry in pairs(tracked) do
                if tool.Parent and entry.cat == cat then
                    local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                    if handle then pcall(buildReplica, handle, key) end
                end
            end
            if saveAmbitiousConfig then pcall(saveAmbitiousConfig) end
        end
        task.spawn(function()
            local n = 0
            while true do
                task.wait(0.4)
                if enabled then
                    n = n + 1
                    pcall(integrityPass, (n % 5) == 0)   -- deep ogni 2s
                end
            end
        end)
        _LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
            task.delay(0.6, function() if enabled then pcall(integrityPass, true) end end)
        end)
        _G.AmbitiousCustomTools = {
            setEnabled = function(on)
                on = on and true or false
                enabled = on
                _G.AmbitiousCustomToolsEnabled = on
                if on then
                    watchAll()
                    pcall(integrityPass, true)
                else
                    for tool in pairs(tracked) do teardown(tool, true) end
                    for _, c in pairs(pendingHandle) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
                    table.clear(pendingHandle)
                    for container in pairs(watchers) do unwatch(container) end
                end
            end,
            isEnabled = function() return enabled end,
        }
    end
    do
        local SOUNDS = {
            Bat    = { id = "rbxassetid://5713085119", skip = 0.2 },
            Medusa = { id = "rbxassetid://3102797479", doublePlay = true, delay = 0.5 },
        }
        local hooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local enabled       = false
        local tracked       = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })  -- [tool] = { sound, data, conns, muted }
        local watchers      = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local pendingHandle = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
        local teardown
        local function playCustom(entry)
            local cs = entry.sound
            if not cs or not cs.Parent then return end
            local d = entry.data
            if entry.timer then
                pcall(task.cancel, entry.timer)
                entry.timer = nil
            end
            pcall(function()
                cs:Stop()
                cs.TimePosition = d.skip or 0
                cs.Volume = 1
                cs:Play()
            end)
            if d.doublePlay and d.delay then
                entry.timer = task.delay(d.delay, function()
                    entry.timer = nil
                    if not enabled then return end
                    if cs and cs.Parent then
                        pcall(function()
                            cs:Stop()
                            cs.TimePosition = d.skip or 0
                            cs.Volume = 1
                            cs:Play()
                        end)
                    end
                end)
            end
        end
        local function hookSound(entry, snd)
            if not snd:IsA("Sound") then return end
            if snd.Name == "CustomSound" then return end
            if hooked[snd] then return end          -- gia' agganciato: niente doppioni
            if entry.muted[snd] ~= nil then return end
            entry.muted[snd] = snd.Volume
            hooked[snd] = true
            local function trigger()
                if not enabled then return end
                if snd.Playing or snd.TimePosition > 0 then
                    pcall(function()
                        snd.Volume = 0
                        snd:Stop()
                    end)
                    playCustom(entry)
                end
            end
            local c1 = snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Playing"):Connect(trigger)
            local c2 = snd:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TimePosition"):Connect(trigger)
            table.insert(entry.conns, c1)
            table.insert(entry.conns, c2)
            local c3
            c3 = snd.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if parent then return end
                pcall(function() c1:Disconnect() end)
                pcall(function() c2:Disconnect() end)
                pcall(function() c3:Disconnect() end)
                hooked[snd] = nil
                entry.muted[snd] = nil
                if entry.timer then
                    pcall(task.cancel, entry.timer)
                    entry.timer = nil
                end
            end)
            table.insert(entry.conns, c3)
            if snd.Playing then trigger() end
        end
        local function setupTool(tool)
            if tracked[tool] then return end
            local item = ctMatchItem(tool)
            if not item then return end
            local data = SOUNDS[item.key]
            if not data then return end
            local handle = tool:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if not handle then
                if pendingHandle[tool] then return end
                local c
                c = tool.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
                    if child.Name == "Handle" then
                        pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                        pendingHandle[tool] = nil
                        if enabled then setupTool(tool) end
                    end
                end)
                pendingHandle[tool] = c
                return
            end
            local old = handle:FindFirstChild("CustomSound")
            if old then pcall(function() old:Destroy() end) end
            local cs = Instance.new("Sound")
            cs.Name        = "CustomSound"
            cs.SoundId     = data.id
            cs.Volume      = 1
            cs.Looped      = false
            cs.RollOffMode = Enum.RollOffMode.Inverse
            cs.MaxDistance = 1000
            cs.MinDistance = 1000
            cs.Parent      = handle
            local entry = { sound = cs, data = data, conns = {}, muted = {} }
            tracked[tool] = entry
            for _, d in ipairs(tool:GetDescendants()) do hookSound(entry, d) end
            table.insert(entry.conns, tool.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
                if tracked[tool] then hookSound(entry, d) end
            end))
            table.insert(entry.conns, tool.AncestryChanged:Connect(function(_, parent)
                if not parent then teardown(tool) end
            end))
        end
        function teardown(tool)
            local entry = tracked[tool]
            if not entry then return end
            if entry.timer then
                pcall(task.cancel, entry.timer)
                entry.timer = nil
            end
            for _, conn in ipairs(entry.conns) do pcall(function() conn:Disconnect() end) end
            if entry.sound then pcall(function() entry.sound:Destroy() end) end
            for snd, vol in pairs(entry.muted) do
                hooked[snd] = nil
                if snd and snd.Parent then pcall(function() snd.Volume = vol end) end
            end
            tracked[tool] = nil
        end
        local function tryRegister(inst)
            if not enabled then return end
            if inst:IsA("Tool") and ctMatchItem(inst) then setupTool(inst) end
        end
        local function unwatch(container)
            local conns = watchers[container]
            if not conns then return end
            for _, c in ipairs(conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            watchers[container] = nil
        end
        local function watch(container)
            if not container or watchers[container] then return end
            watchers[container] = { container.ChildAdded:Connect(tryRegister) }
            for _, child in ipairs(container:GetChildren()) do tryRegister(child) end
        end
        local function watchAll()
            watch(_LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack"))
            watch(_LP.Character)
        end
        _LP.ChildAdded:Connect(function(child)
            if enabled and child:IsA("Backpack") then watch(child) end
        end)
        _LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
            for tool in pairs(tracked) do teardown(tool) end
            for _, c in pairs(pendingHandle) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
            table.clear(pendingHandle)
            for container in pairs(watchers) do unwatch(container) end
            if not enabled then return end
            watch(char)
            task.defer(function()
                if enabled then watchAll() end
            end)
        end)
        table.insert(CT_JANITORS, function()
            for tool in pairs(tracked) do
                if not tool.Parent then teardown(tool) end
            end
            for tool, c in pairs(pendingHandle) do
                if not tool.Parent then
                    pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
                    pendingHandle[tool] = nil
                end
            end
            for container in pairs(watchers) do
                if not container.Parent then unwatch(container) end
            end
        end)
        _G.AmbitiousCustomSounds = {
            setEnabled = function(on)
                on = on and true or false
                enabled = on
                _G.AmbitiousCustomSoundsEnabled = on
                if on then
                    watchAll()
                else
                    for tool in pairs(tracked) do teardown(tool) end
                    for _, c in pairs(pendingHandle) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
                    table.clear(pendingHandle)
                    for container in pairs(watchers) do unwatch(container) end
                end
            end,
            isEnabled = function() return enabled end,
        }
    end
    _G.AmbitiousRainbowToolsEnabled     = false
    _G.AmbitiousTransparentToolsEnabled = false
    _G.AmbitiousCustomToolsEnabled      = false
    _G.AmbitiousCustomSoundsEnabled     = false
end

local removeAccessoriesEnabled = false
_G._AmbitiousDefLightBrightness = _G._AmbitiousDefLightBrightness or nil

local unwalkEnabled = false

function _G.AmbitiousHasCustomAnim()
  for _, slot in ipairs(_G.AmbitiousCustomAnimSlots) do
    local v = _G.AmbitiousCustomAnimIds[slot]
    if v and v ~= "" then return true end
  end
  return false
end

local listeningForSpeedKey = nil
