      cardRefs = {}
      for i, slot in ipairs(SLOTS) do

        nameLbl.ZIndex = 5

        mark.BackgroundTransparency = 1

          TweenService:Create(clear, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 110)}):Play()

          TweenService:Create(clear, TweenInfo.new(0.1), {TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 55, 55)}):Play()

        clear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
          _G.AmbitiousCustomAnimIds[slot] = ""
          refreshCard(slot)
          saveAmbitiousConfig()
          if _G.AmbitiousReloadCustomAnimPack then
            pcall(_G.AmbitiousReloadCustomAnimPack)
          end
        end)
        card.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
          tween(card, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 28)}, 0.12)
        end)
        card.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
          tween(card, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18)}, 0.12)
        end)
        card.Activated:Connect(function() openAssetPopup(slot) end)
        cardRefs[slot] = {mark = mark, sub = sub, stroke = cs, clear = clear}
        refreshCard(slot)
      end
      frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
      frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
      TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 380, 0, 336),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -190, 0.5, -168)
      }):Play()
    end)
  end)()
  section(Utility, "AVATAR", 11)
  do
    _G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger = {
      Hair     = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Hats     = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Face     = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Neck     = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Shoulder = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Front    = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Back     = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Waist    = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Shirts   = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Pants    = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      ["T-Shirts"] = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Jackets  = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Sweaters = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
      Bottoms  = { enabled=false, savedId=nil, savedName="" },
    }
    if type(_G._AmbitiousAvatarChangerPending) == "table" then
      for k,v in pairs(_G._AmbitiousAvatarChangerPending) do
        if _G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[k] then
          _G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[k].enabled   = v.enabled == true
          _G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[k].savedId   = tonumber(v.savedId)
          _G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[k].savedName = tostring(v.savedName or "")
        end
      end
      _G._AmbitiousAvatarChangerPending = nil
    end
    local AC_CATEGORIES = {
      { Name="Hair",     AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.HairAccessory} },
      { Name="Hats",     AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.Hat} },
      { Name="Face",     AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.FaceAccessory} },
      { Name="Neck",     AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.NeckAccessory} },
      { Name="Shoulder", AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.ShoulderAccessory} },
      { Name="Front",    AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.FrontAccessory} },
      { Name="Back",     AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.BackAccessory} },
      { Name="Waist",    AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.WaistAccessory} },
      { Name="Shirts",   AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.Shirt} },
      { Name="Pants",    AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.Pants} },
      { Name="T-Shirts", AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.TShirt} },
      { Name="Jackets",  AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.JacketAccessory} },
      { Name="Sweaters", AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.SweaterAccessory} },
      { Name="Bottoms",  AssetTypes={Enum.AvatarAssetType.ShortsAccessory} },
    }
    local _acGen = 0
    local _acAssetCache = {}
    local _acWaitCharReady = _G._AmbitiousWaitCharReady
    local function _acEquipItem(itemId, itemType, itemName, gen)
      if not itemId then return false end
      if gen == nil then gen = _acGen end
      local ok, applied = pcall(function()
        local objects
        local cached = _acAssetCache[itemId]
        if cached then
          objects = { cached:Clone() }
        else
          objects = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://"..tostring(itemId))
          if objects and objects[1] then
            _acAssetCache[itemId] = objects[1]:Clone()
          end
        end
        if gen ~= _acGen then return false end
        local character = LP.Character
        if not character or not character.Parent then return false end
        if not _acWaitCharReady(character, 5) then return false end
        if gen ~= _acGen then return false end
        if not objects or #objects==0 then return false end
          local item = objects[1]
          if item:IsA("Folder") or item:IsA("Model") then
            local core = item:FindFirstChildOfClass("Accessory")
              or item:FindFirstChildOfClass("Shirt")
              or item:FindFirstChildOfClass("Pants")
              or item:FindFirstChildOfClass("ShirtGraphic")
            if core then item=core end
          end
          if item:IsA("Shirt") then
            for _,c in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("Shirt") then c:Destroy() end end
            item.Name="ACShirt"; item.Parent=character; return true
          elseif item:IsA("Pants") then
            for _,c in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("Pants") then c:Destroy() end end
            item.Name="ACPants"; item.Parent=character; return true
          elseif item:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
            for _,c in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then c:Destroy() end end
            item.Name="ACTShirt"; item.Parent=character; return true
          end
          if item:IsA("Accessory") then
            local handle=item:FindFirstChild("Handle")
            if handle then
              if itemType==Enum.AvatarAssetType.HairAccessory then
                for _,c in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                  if c:IsA("Accessory") and (c.Name:lower():find("hair") or (c:FindFirstChild("Handle") and c.Handle:FindFirstChild("HairAttachment"))) then c:Destroy() end
                end
              else
                for _,c in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                  if c:IsA("Accessory") and (c.Name==itemName or c.Name=="AC"..itemName) then c:Destroy() end
                end
              end
              local attachment=handle:FindFirstChildOfClass("Attachment")
              local targetPart=character:FindFirstChild("Head")
              local targetAttachment=nil
              if attachment then
                for _,bodyPart in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
                  if bodyPart:IsA("BasePart") then
                    targetAttachment=bodyPart:FindFirstChild(attachment.Name)
                    if targetAttachment then targetPart=bodyPart; break end
                  end
                end
              end
              if not targetPart then
                targetPart = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
              end
              if not targetPart then return false end
              item.Name="AC"..itemName; item.Parent=character
              handle.CFrame=targetPart.CFrame
              for _,j in pairs(handle:GetChildren()) do
                if j:IsA("Weld") or j:IsA("ManualWeld") or j:IsA("WeldConstraint") then j:Destroy() end
              end
              local w=Instance.new("Weld")
              w.Name="ACManualWeld"; w.Part0=handle; w.Part1=targetPart
              if attachment and targetAttachment then w.C0=attachment.CFrame; w.C1=targetAttachment.CFrame
              else w.C0=CFrame.new(0,0.5,0) end
              w.Parent=handle
              return true
            end
          end
          return false
      end)
      return ok and applied == true
    end
    local function _acRemoveItem(itemType, itemName)
      local character = LP.Character
      if not character then return end
      pcall(function()
        if itemType == Enum.AvatarAssetType.Shirt then
          for _,c in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("Shirt") and (c.Name=="ACShirt" or c.Name==itemName) then c:Destroy() end
          end
        elseif itemType == Enum.AvatarAssetType.Pants then
          for _,c in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("Pants") and (c.Name=="ACPants" or c.Name==itemName) then c:Destroy() end
          end
        elseif itemType == Enum.AvatarAssetType.TShirt then
          for _,c in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("ShirtGraphic") and (c.Name=="ACTShirt" or c.Name==itemName) then c:Destroy() end
          end
        else
          for _,c in pairs(character:GetChildren()) do
            if c:IsA("Accessory") and (c.Name=="AC"..itemName or c.Name==itemName) then c:Destroy() end
          end
        end
      end)
    end
    local function _acSaveConfig()
      pcall(saveAmbitiousConfig)
    end
    local function _acApplyAll(gen)
      if gen == nil then gen = _acGen end
      local character = LP.Character
      if not character then return end
      if not _acWaitCharReady(character, 10) then return end
      if gen ~= _acGen then return end
      for _,cat in ipairs(AC_CATEGORIES) do
        if gen ~= _acGen then return end   -- respawn nel frattempo: si abortisce
        local entry=_G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[cat.Name]
        if entry and entry.enabled and entry.savedId then
          for attempt = 1, 3 do
            if gen ~= _acGen then return end
            if _acEquipItem(entry.savedId, cat.AssetTypes[1], entry.savedName, gen) then
              break
            end
            task.wait(0.25 * attempt)
          end
          task.wait(0.05)
        end
      end
    end
    task.spawn(function()
      if not LP.Character then return end
      _acApplyAll(_acGen)
    end)
    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
      _acGen = _acGen + 1          -- invalida gli equip del character precedente
      local gen = _acGen
      task.spawn(function()
        _acApplyAll(gen)
      end)
    end)
    ;(function()
      local lastTry   = {}
      local fails     = {}
      local charConns = {}
      local pendingCheck = false
      local runCheck
      local function requestCheck(delaySec)
        if pendingCheck then return end
        pendingCheck = true
        task.delay(delaySec or 0.1, function()
          pendingCheck = false
          pcall(runCheck)
        end)
      end
      local function expectedFor(cat, entry)
        local at = cat.AssetTypes[1]
        if at == Enum.AvatarAssetType.Shirt then
          return "ACShirt", "Shirt"
        elseif at == Enum.AvatarAssetType.Pants then
          return "ACPants", "Pants"
        elseif at == Enum.AvatarAssetType.TShirt then
          return "ACTShirt", "ShirtGraphic"
        end
        return "AC"..tostring(entry.savedName or ""), "Accessory"
      end
      local function isPresent(character, cat, entry)
        local nm, cls = expectedFor(cat, entry)
        for _, c in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
          if c:IsA(cls) and (c.Name == nm or c.Name == entry.savedName) then
            return true
          end
        end
        return false
      end
      function runCheck()
        local character = LP.Character
        if not character or not character.Parent then return end
        local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.Health <= 0 then return end
        local now = tick()
        for _, cat in ipairs(AC_CATEGORIES) do
          local entry = _G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[cat.Name]
          if entry and entry.enabled and entry.savedId then
            if isPresent(character, cat, entry) then
              fails[cat.Name] = nil
            else
              local n = fails[cat.Name] or 0
              local wait = math.min(0.6 * (2 ^ math.min(n, 5)), 20)
              if (now - (lastTry[cat.Name] or 0)) > wait then
                lastTry[cat.Name] = now
                local gen = _acGen
                local _cn = cat.Name
                local _id, _at, _nm = entry.savedId, cat.AssetTypes[1], entry.savedName
                task.spawn(function()
                  if gen ~= _acGen then return end
                  if _acEquipItem(_id, _at, _nm, gen) then
                    fails[_cn] = nil
                  else
                    fails[_cn] = (fails[_cn] or 0) + 1
                  end
                end)
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
      _G._AmbitiousAvatarChangerRecheck = function() fails = {}; pcall(runCheck) end
      local function hookCharacter(character)
        for _, c in ipairs(charConns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
        charConns = {}
        if not character then return end
        table.insert(charConns, character.ChildRemoved:Connect(function(child)
          if child:IsA("Accessory") or child:IsA("Shirt")
          or child:IsA("Pants") or child:IsA("ShirtGraphic") then
            requestCheck(0.05)
          end
        end))
        local hum = character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
          table.insert(charConns, hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function()
            requestCheck(0.15)
          end))
        end
      end
      hookCharacter(LP.Character)
      LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c)
        task.delay(0.6, function() hookCharacter(c) end)
      end)
      task.spawn(function()
        while true do
          task.wait(1)
          pcall(runCheck)
        end
      end)
    end)()
    local _acSubSetVisuals = {}
    local _acPickerGui = nil
    local _acPickerCat = nil
    local _acPickerJustClosedCat = nil
    local _acPickerJustClosedAt = 0
    local function _acClosePicker()
      if _acPickerGui and _acPickerGui.Parent then
        _acPickerGui:Destroy()
        _acPickerJustClosedCat = _acPickerCat
        _acPickerJustClosedAt = tick()
      end
      _acPickerGui = nil
      _acPickerCat = nil
    end
    local function _acOpenPicker(catName, assetTypes)
      if _acPickerGui and _acPickerGui.Parent and _acPickerCat == catName then
        _acClosePicker()
        return
      end
      if _acPickerJustClosedCat == catName and (tick() - _acPickerJustClosedAt) < 0.25 then
        _acPickerJustClosedCat = nil
        return
      end
      _acClosePicker()
      _acPickerJustClosedCat = nil
      _acPickerCat = catName
      _acPickerGui=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
      _acPickerGui.Name="AmbitiousHubAVChanger"
      _acPickerGui.ResetOnSpawn=false
      _acPickerGui.IgnoreGuiInset=true
      _acPickerGui.ZIndexBehavior=Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
      _acPickerGui.DisplayOrder=9998
      _acPickerGui.Parent=HubGui
      local backdrop=Instance.new("Frame")
      backdrop.Name="Backdrop"
      backdrop.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
      backdrop.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
      backdrop.BackgroundTransparency=0.45
      backdrop.BorderSizePixel=0
      backdrop.ZIndex=1
      backdrop.Parent=_acPickerGui
      local pickerFrame=Instance.new("Frame")
      pickerFrame.Name="Frame"
      pickerFrame.Size=UDim2.new(0,370,0,510)
      pickerFrame.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-185,0.5,-255)
      pickerFrame.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(8,8,10)
      pickerFrame.BorderSizePixel=0
      pickerFrame.ZIndex=2
      pickerFrame.Parent=_acPickerGui
      corner(pickerFrame,16)
      stroke(pickerFrame,Color3.fromRGB(90,90,105),1.2,0.3)
      local titleBar=Instance.new("Frame")
      titleBar.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,48)
      titleBar.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(12,12,15)
      titleBar.BackgroundTransparency=0.15
      titleBar.BorderSizePixel=0
      titleBar.ZIndex=3
      titleBar.Parent=pickerFrame
      corner(titleBar,16)
      local titleLabel=Instance.new("TextLabel")
      titleLabel.Size=UDim2.new(1,-60,1,0)
      titleLabel.Position=UDim2.new(0,18,0,0)
      titleLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1
      titleLabel.Text=catName.." Changer"
      titleLabel.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
      titleLabel.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
      titleLabel.TextSize=15
      titleLabel.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
      titleLabel.ZIndex=4
      titleLabel.Parent=titleBar
      local closeBtn=Instance.new("TextButton")
      closeBtn.Size=UDim2.new(0,36,0,36)
      closeBtn.Position=UDim2.new(1,-44,0,6)
      closeBtn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(25,25,28)
      closeBtn.BackgroundTransparency=0.1
      closeBtn.BorderSizePixel=0
      closeBtn.Text="X"
      closeBtn.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
      closeBtn.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack
      closeBtn.TextSize=14
      closeBtn.AutoButtonColor=false
      closeBtn.ZIndex=4
      closeBtn.Parent=titleBar
      corner(closeBtn,10)
      stroke(closeBtn,Color3.fromRGB(90,90,105),1,0.4)
      closeBtn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(closeBtn,{BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(180,50,50)},0.12) end)
      closeBtn.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(closeBtn,{BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(25,25,28)},0.12) end)
      closeBtn.Activated:Connect(_acClosePicker)
      local searchBar=Instance.new("TextBox")
      searchBar.Size=UDim2.new(1,-24,0,30)
      searchBar.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0,55)
      searchBar.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(18,18,22)
      searchBar.BackgroundTransparency=0.1
      searchBar.BorderSizePixel=0
      searchBar.PlaceholderText="Search..."
      searchBar.PlaceholderColor3=Color3.fromRGB(110,110,120)
      searchBar.Text=""
      searchBar.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(245,245,255)
      searchBar.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
      searchBar.TextSize=12
      searchBar.ClearTextOnFocus=false
      searchBar.ZIndex=4
      searchBar.Parent=pickerFrame
      corner(searchBar,8)
      stroke(searchBar,Color3.fromRGB(70,70,85),1,0.4)
      local statusLabel=Instance.new("TextLabel")
      statusLabel.Size=UDim2.new(1,-24,0,22)
      statusLabel.Position=UDim2.new(0,12,0,92)
      statusLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1
      statusLabel.Text="Loading..."
      statusLabel.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(150,150,160)
      statusLabel.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
      statusLabel.TextSize=11
      statusLabel.ZIndex=4
      statusLabel.Parent=pickerFrame
      local scrollFrame=Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
      scrollFrame.Size=UDim2.new(1,-12,1,-100)
      scrollFrame.Position=UDim2.new(0,6,0,96)
      scrollFrame.BackgroundTransparency=1
      scrollFrame.BorderSizePixel=0
      scrollFrame.ScrollBarThickness=4
      scrollFrame.ScrollBarImageColor3=Color3.fromRGB(80,80,90)
      scrollFrame.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
      scrollFrame.AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
      scrollFrame.ZIndex=3
      scrollFrame.Parent=pickerFrame
      local gridLayout=Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
      gridLayout.CellSize=UDim2.new(0,100,0,120)
      gridLayout.CellPadding=UDim2.new(0,8,0,8)
      gridLayout.FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
      gridLayout.HorizontalAlignment=Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
      gridLayout.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

      local itemOrder=0
      local currentCatalogPage=nil
      local loadingMore=false
      local function makeItemCard(item)
        itemOrder=itemOrder+1
        local card=Instance.new("TextButton")
        card.Name="AC_Item_"..tostring(item.Id)
        card.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(16,16,20)
        card.BackgroundTransparency=0.05
        card.BorderSizePixel=0
        card.Text=""
        card.AutoButtonColor=false
        card.LayoutOrder=itemOrder
        card.ZIndex=4
        card.Parent=scrollFrame
        corner(card,10)
        local cardStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke")
        cardStroke.ApplyStrokeMode=Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        cardStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(50,50,62)
        cardStroke.Thickness=1
        cardStroke.Transparency=0.5
        cardStroke.Parent=card
        local thumb=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        thumb.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,72)
        thumb.Position=UDim2.new(0,4,0,4)
        thumb.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(22,22,28)
        thumb.BorderSizePixel=0
        thumb.ZIndex=5
        thumb.Image="rbxthumb://type=Asset&id="..tostring(item.Id).."&w=150&h=150"
        thumb.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Fit
        thumb.Parent=card
        corner(thumb,7)
        local nameLabel=Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLabel.Size=UDim2.new(1,-6,0,30)
        nameLabel.Position=UDim2.new(0,3,0,78)
        nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1
        nameLabel.Text=item.Name or "?"
        nameLabel.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(225,225,235)
        nameLabel.Font=Enum.Font.GothamSemibold
        nameLabel.TextSize=9
        nameLabel.TextWrapped=true
        nameLabel.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
        nameLabel.TextYAlignment=Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
        nameLabel.ZIndex=5
        nameLabel.Parent=card
        card.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
          tween(card,{BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(30,30,38)},0.1)
          tween(cardStroke,{Color=Color3.fromRGB(200,200,210),Transparency=0.15},0.1)
        end)
        card.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
          local entry=_G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[catName]
          local isSaved = entry and entry.savedId == item.Id
          tween(card,{BackgroundColor3=isSaved and Color3.fromRGB(20,36,20) or Color3.fromRGB(16,16,20)},0.1)
          tween(cardStroke,{Color=isSaved and Color3.fromRGB(120,220,120) or Color3.fromRGB(50,50,62),Transparency=isSaved and 0.15 or 0.5},0.1)
        end)
        local entry=_G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[catName]
        if entry and entry.savedId == item.Id then
          card.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(20,36,20)
          cardStroke.Color=Color3.fromRGB(120,220,120)
          cardStroke.Transparency=0.15
        end
        card.Activated:Connect(function()
          local ent=_G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[catName]
          if not ent then return end
          if ent.enabled and ent.savedId and ent.savedName ~= "" then
            _acRemoveItem(assetTypes[1], ent.savedName)
          end
          ent.savedId=item.Id
          ent.savedName=item.Name or ""
          if ent.enabled then
            local _id, _at, _nm = item.Id, assetTypes[1], item.Name or ""
            task.spawn(function() _acEquipItem(_id,_at,_nm) end)
          end
          _acSaveConfig()
          for _,ch in ipairs(scrollFrame:GetChildren()) do
            if ch:IsA("TextButton") then
              local st2=ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
              local isThis=(ch.Name=="AC_Item_"..tostring(item.Id))
              tween(ch,{BackgroundColor3=isThis and Color3.fromRGB(20,36,20) or Color3.fromRGB(16,16,20)},0.14)
              if st2 then tween(st2,{Color=isThis and Color3.fromRGB(120,220,120) or Color3.fromRGB(50,50,62),Transparency=isThis and 0.15 or 0.5},0.14) end
            end
          end
        end)
      end
      local function loadItems(keyword)
        for _,c in ipairs(scrollFrame:GetChildren()) do if c:IsA("TextButton") then c:Destroy() end end
        itemOrder=0
        currentCatalogPage=nil
        loadingMore=false
        statusLabel.Text="Loading..."
        statusLabel.Visible=true
        task.spawn(function()
          local ok,pages2=pcall(function()
            local params=CatalogSearchParams.new()
            params.AssetTypes=assetTypes
            if keyword and keyword~="" then params.SearchKeyword=keyword end
            return game:GetService("AvatarEditorService"):SearchCatalog(params)
          end)
          if not ok or not pages2 then statusLabel.Text="Error loading."; return end
          currentCatalogPage=pages2
          local firstPage=pages2:GetCurrentPage()
          statusLabel.Visible=#firstPage==0
          statusLabel.Text=#firstPage==0 and "No results." or ""
          for _,item in ipairs(firstPage) do makeItemCard(item) end
        end)
      end
      scrollFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(function()
        if loadingMore or not currentCatalogPage then return end
        local posY=scrollFrame.CanvasPosition.Y
        local canvasH=scrollFrame.CanvasSize.Y.Offset
        local viewH=scrollFrame.AbsoluteSize.Y
        if canvasH>0 and posY+viewH>=canvasH-100 and not currentCatalogPage.IsFinished then
          loadingMore=true
          task.spawn(function()
            local ok2=pcall(function() currentCatalogPage:AdvanceToNextPageAsync() end)
            if ok2 then
              for _,item in ipairs(currentCatalogPage:GetCurrentPage()) do makeItemCard(item) end
            end
            loadingMore=false
          end)
        end
      end)
      local searchDebounce=nil
      searchBar:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
        if searchDebounce then task.cancel(searchDebounce) end
        searchDebounce=task.delay(0.5,function() loadItems(searchBar.Text) end)
      end)
      backdrop.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
          _acClosePicker()
        end
      end)
      pickerFrame.Size=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
      pickerFrame.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,0,0.5,0)
      TweenService:Create(pickerFrame,TweenInfo.new(0.3,Enum.EasingStyle.Back,Enum.EasingDirection.Out),{
        Size=UDim2.new(0,370,0,510),
        Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-185,0.5,-255)
      }):Play()
      loadItems("")
    end
    local acMainRow=baseRow(Utility,"Avatar Changer",11.5)
    acMainRow.LayoutOrder=11.5
    local acArrowBtn=Instance.new("TextButton")
    acArrowBtn.Name="AvatarChangerArrow"

      for _,cat in ipairs(AC_CATEGORIES) do
        local entry=_G._AmbitiousAvatarChanger[cat.Name]
        if entry then
          if entry.enabled and entry.savedId then
            _acRemoveItem(cat.AssetTypes[1], entry.savedName)
          end
          entry.enabled=false; entry.savedId=nil; entry.savedName=""

        end
      end

    local ctGui = nil
    local function closeCTPicker()
      if ctGui and ctGui.Parent then
        local fr = ctGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        if fr then TweenService:Create(fr, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play() end
        task.delay(0.25, function()
          if ctGui and ctGui.Parent then ctGui:Destroy() end
          ctGui = nil
        end)
      end
    end
    ctPickBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
      if ctGui and ctGui.Parent then closeCTPicker() return end

      local function buildCard(def, key, idx)
        local card = Instance.new("Frame")
        card.Name = "Skin_" .. key
        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18)
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15

        vp.Name = "Preview"
        vp.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 11)
        vp.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        vp.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 116)
        vp.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
        vp.BorderSizePixel = 0
        vp.ZIndex = 5

        nameLbl.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
        nameLbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)

        click.AutoButtonColor = false
        click.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        click.ZIndex = 8
        click.Parent = card

                        part = part, stroke = cStroke, idx = idx,
                        vp = vp, cam = cam, def = def }
        local dist = def.view or 4
        cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0, dist * 0.35, dist), Vector3.new(0, 0, 0))
        local function refreshSel()
          local sel = (_G.AmbitiousCustomToolSkins[def.cat] == key)
          tween(cStroke, {
            Color = sel and COLORS.white or COLORS.strokeSoft,
            Transparency = sel and 0 or 0.5,
            Thickness = sel and 2.2 or 1.3,
          }, 0.14)
          tween(card, {BackgroundTransparency = sel and 0 or 0.15}, 0.14)
        end
        entry.refresh = refreshSel
        click.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
          local w = entry.baseW or card.Size.X.Offset
          local x = entry.baseX or card.Position.X.Offset
          tween(card, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, w + 4, 0, 176),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, x - 2, 0, 8),
          }, 0.12)
        end)
        click.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
          local w = entry.baseW or card.Size.X.Offset
          local x = entry.baseX or card.Position.X.Offset
          tween(card, {
            Size = UDim2.new(0, w, 0, 172),
            Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 10),

        end)

          if _G.AmbitiousSetCustomToolSkin then
            _G.AmbitiousSetCustomToolSkin(def.cat, key)
          end
          for _, e in ipairs(cards) do e.refresh() end
        end)
        refreshSel()
        return entry
      end
      for key, def in pairs(_G.AmbitiousCustomToolAssets or {}) do

      spinConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        for _, e in ipairs(cards) do
          if e.card.Visible and e.part.Parent then
            e.part.CFrame = e.part.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0, dt * 1.2, 0)
          end

        if not parent and spinConn then
          pcall(function() spinConn:Disconnect() end)
          spinConn = nil
        end

      local current = "Bat"
      local PAGE_W = 420 - 24
      local GAP = 14
      local INSET = 9
      local function layoutCat(cat, fromRight, animate)
        local list = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(cards) do
          if e.cat == cat then list[#list + 1] = e end
        end
        local n = #list
        if n == 0 then return list end
        local cardW = math.floor((PAGE_W - INSET * 2 - GAP * (n - 1)) / n)
        for i, e in ipairs(list) do
          local targetX = INSET + (i - 1) * (cardW + GAP)
          e.card.Size = UDim2.new(0, cardW, 0, 172)
          e.baseW = cardW
          e.baseX = targetX
          e.card.Visible = true
          if animate then
            e.card.Position = UDim2.new(0, targetX + (fromRight and 300 or -300), 0, 10)
            tween(e.card, {Position = UDim2.new(0, targetX, 0, 10)}, 0.24)
          else
            e.card.Position = UDim2.new(0, targetX, 0, 10)
          end
        end
        return list
      end
      local switchToken = 0
      local function setCat(cat, animate)
        if cat == current and animate then return end
        local goingRight = (cat == "Medusa")
        switchToken = switchToken + 1
        local myToken = switchToken
        local outList = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(cards) do
          if e.cat ~= cat and e.card.Visible then outList[#outList + 1] = e end
        end
        current = cat
        if animate then
          for _, e in ipairs(outList) do
            local x = e.card.Position.X.Offset
            tween(e.card, {Position = UDim2.new(0, x + (goingRight and -300 or 300), 0, 10)}, 0.18)
          end
          task.delay(0.14, function()
            if myToken ~= switchToken then return end
            for _, e in ipairs(outList) do
              if e.card.Parent then e.card.Visible = false end
            end
          end)
        else
          for _, e in ipairs(outList) do e.card.Visible = false end
        end
        layoutCat(cat, goingRight, animate)
        local idx = (cat == "Bat") and 1 or 2
        tween(slide, {Position = UDim2.new(0.25 + 0.5 * (idx - 1), 0, 0.5, 0)}, 0.2)
        for i, lbl in ipairs(catLabels) do
          tween(lbl, {
            TextColor3 = (i == idx) and Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 28) or Color3.fromRGB(225, 225, 235),
            TextTransparency = (i == idx) and 0 or 0.35,
          }, 0.16)
        end
      end
      for i, c in ipairs(CATS) do
        local clickTab = Instance.new("TextButton")
        clickTab.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        clickTab.Text = ""
        clickTab.AutoButtonColor = false
        clickTab.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        clickTab.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 1, 0)
        clickTab.Position = UDim2.new(0.25 + 0.5 * (i - 1), 0, 0.5, 0)
        clickTab.ZIndex = 8
        clickTab.Parent = tabsHolder
        local key = c.key
        clickTab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setCat(key, true) end)
      end
      backdrop.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1
           or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
          closeCTPicker()
        end
      end)
      task.defer(function()
        for _, e in ipairs(cards) do e.card.Visible = false end
        setCat("Bat", false)
      end)
      frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
      frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
      TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 470),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -210, 0.5, -235)
      }):Play()
    end)
  end

    local rowRT, setVisualRT = toggleRow(Utility, "Rainbow Tools", _G.AmbitiousRainbowToolsEnabled == true, 12)
    _SV.setRainbowToolsVisual = setVisualRT

      btn.Activated:Connect(function()
        local on = not (_G.AmbitiousRainbowToolsEnabled == true)
        if _G.AmbitiousRainbowTools then _G.AmbitiousRainbowTools.setEnabled(on) end
        if setVisualRT then setVisualRT(on) end
        saveAmbitiousConfig()
      end)
    end
  end
  do
    local rowTT, setVisualTT = toggleRow(Utility, "Transparent Tools", _G.AmbitiousTransparentToolsEnabled == true, 12)
    _SV.setTransparentToolsVisual = setVisualTT
    local btn = rowTT and rowTT:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")

        local on = not (_G.AmbitiousTransparentToolsEnabled == true)
        if _G.AmbitiousTransparentTools then _G.AmbitiousTransparentTools.setEnabled(on) end
        if setVisualTT then setVisualTT(on) end
        saveAmbitiousConfig()
      end)
    end
  end

    _SV.setCustomSoundsVisual = setVisualCS

        if setVisualCS then setVisualCS(on) end

    end
  end
section(Utility, "CAMERA", 13)
do
    local STRETCH_PICKS = { {idx = 2, name = "X1.5"}, {idx = 3, name = "X2"}, {idx = 4, name = "X3"} }
    local SEG_N = #STRETCH_PICKS
    _G.AmbitiousStretchPick = _G.AmbitiousStretchPick
        or ((stretchCurrentIndex and stretchCurrentIndex > 1) and stretchCurrentIndex or 2)
    local row, setStretchVisual = toggleRow(Utility, "Stretch Rez", stretchEnabled == true, 14)
    local segHolder = Instance.new("Frame")
    segHolder.Name = "Stretch Rez Options"
    segHolder.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.row
    segHolder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.28
    segHolder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 42)
    segHolder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    segHolder.LayoutOrder = 14
    segHolder.ZIndex = 4
    segHolder.ClipsDescendants = true
    segHolder.Visible = false
    segHolder.Parent = Utility
    corner(segHolder, 14)
    stroke(segHolder, Color3.fromRGB(126, 71, 190), 1.4, 0.32)
    _G._AmbitiousRegAccent(segHolder, nil, "soft")
    local segSlide = Instance.new("Frame")
    segSlide.Name = "SelectedSlide"
    segSlide.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(147, 51, 234)
    segSlide.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    segSlide.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
    segSlide.Size = UDim2.new(1 / SEG_N, -4, 1, -8)
    segSlide.Position = UDim2.new(0.5 / SEG_N, 0, 0.5, 0)
    segSlide.BorderSizePixel = 0
    segSlide.ZIndex = 5
    segSlide.Parent = segHolder
    corner(segSlide, 13)
    local segSlideStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    segSlideStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    segSlideStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(216, 180, 254)
    segSlideStroke.Thickness = 1.5
    segSlideStroke.Transparency = 0.1
    segSlideStroke.Parent = segSlide
    local segSlideGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
    segSlideGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(202, 148, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(147, 51, 234)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(101, 31, 190)),
    })
    segSlideGrad.Rotation = 22
    segSlideGrad.Parent = segSlide
    _G._AmbitiousRegAccent(segSlide, segSlideGrad, "slide")
    local segLabels = {}
    local segW = 1 / SEG_N
    for i, p in ipairs(STRETCH_PICKS) do
        local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        lbl.Text = p.name
        lbl.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
        lbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        lbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
        lbl.TextSize = 11
        lbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        lbl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        lbl.Size = UDim2.new(segW, 0, 1, 0)
        lbl.Position = UDim2.new(segW * (i - 0.5), 0, 0.5, 0)
        lbl.ZIndex = 8
        lbl.Parent = segHolder
        segLabels[i] = lbl
        local clk = Instance.new("TextButton")
        clk.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        clk.Text = ""
        clk.AutoButtonColor = false
        clk.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        clk.Size = UDim2.new(segW, 0, 1, 0)
        clk.Position = UDim2.new(segW * (i - 0.5), 0, 0.5, 0)
        clk.ZIndex = 10
        clk.Parent = segHolder
        local picked = p.idx
        clk.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            _G.AmbitiousStretchPick = picked
            if stretchEnabled then applyStretchPreset(picked) end
            if _G.AmbitiousRefreshStretchSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshStretchSelector() end
            saveAmbitiousConfig()
        end)
    end
    local function refreshStretchSelector()
        local pick = _G.AmbitiousStretchPick or 2
        local segIdx = 1
        for i, p in ipairs(STRETCH_PICKS) do
            if p.idx == pick then segIdx = i break end
        end
        tween(segSlide, {
            Position = UDim2.new(segW * (segIdx - 0.5), 0, 0.5, 0),
            Size = UDim2.new(segW, -4, 1, -8),
        }, 0.18)
        for i, lbl in ipairs(segLabels) do
            tween(lbl, {
                TextTransparency = (i == segIdx) and 0 or 0.45,
                TextStrokeTransparency = (i == segIdx) and 0.2 or 0.45,
            }, 0.14)
        end
        if setStretchVisual then pcall(setStretchVisual, stretchEnabled == true) end
    end
    _G.AmbitiousRefreshStretchSelector = refreshStretchSelector
    local sBtn = row and row:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")
    if sBtn then
        sBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
            if stretchEnabled then
                applyStretchPreset(1)                       -- spento = X1
            else
                applyStretchPreset(_G.AmbitiousStretchPick or 2)
            end
            refreshStretchSelector()
            saveAmbitiousConfig()
        end)
    end
    local sArrow = Instance.new("TextButton")
    sArrow.Name = "StretchRezArrow"
    sArrow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
    sArrow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.18
    sArrow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    sArrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 24)
    sArrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0.5, -12)
    sArrow.Text = "\u{25B2}"
    sArrow.TextColor3 = COLORS.white
    sArrow.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    sArrow.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
    sArrow.TextSize = 15
    sArrow.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    sArrow.AutoButtonColor = false
    sArrow.ZIndex = 8
    sArrow.Parent = row
    corner(sArrow, 7)
    stroke(sArrow, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.45)
    local sExpanded = false
    sArrow.Activated:Connect(function()
        sExpanded = not sExpanded
        _G._AmbitiousExpand(segHolder, sExpanded, 42, sArrow)
    end)
    refreshStretchSelector()
end
do
    local function _startMotionBlur()
        if _motionBlurConn then pcall(function() _motionBlurConn:Disconnect() end); _motionBlurConn = nil end
        if not _motionBlurEffect or not _motionBlurEffect.Parent then
            _motionBlurEffect = Lighting:FindFirstChildOfClass("BlurEffect")
            if not _motionBlurEffect then
                _motionBlurEffect = Instance.new("BlurEffect")
                _motionBlurEffect.Name = "AmbitiousMotionBlur"
            end
        end
        _motionBlurEffect.Size = 0
        _motionBlurEffect.Enabled = true
        _motionBlurEffect.Parent = Lighting
        local camera = Workspace.CurrentCamera
        local lastCFrame = camera.CFrame
        _motionBlurCurrentValue = 0
        _motionBlurConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(deltaTime)
            if not motionBlurEnabled then
                if _motionBlurConn then pcall(function() _motionBlurConn:Disconnect() end); _motionBlurConn = nil end
                if _motionBlurEffect and _motionBlurEffect.Parent then
                    _motionBlurEffect.Size = 0
                    _motionBlurEffect.Enabled = false
                end
                return
            end
            local cam = Workspace.CurrentCamera
            if not cam then return end
            local currentCF = cam.CFrame
            local dot = currentCF.LookVector:Dot(lastCFrame.LookVector)
            local angleRad = math.acos(math.clamp(dot, -1, 1))
            local angleDeg = math.deg(angleRad)
            local speed = angleDeg * 20
            local targetBlur = math.min(speed, 7)
            _motionBlurCurrentValue = _motionBlurCurrentValue + (targetBlur - _motionBlurCurrentValue) * math.min(1, deltaTime * 10)
            if _motionBlurEffect and _motionBlurEffect.Parent then
                _motionBlurEffect.Size = _motionBlurCurrentValue
            end
            lastCFrame = currentCF
        end)
    end
    local function _stopMotionBlur()
        if _motionBlurConn then pcall(function() _motionBlurConn:Disconnect() end); _motionBlurConn = nil end
        if _motionBlurEffect and _motionBlurEffect.Parent then
            _motionBlurEffect.Size = 0
            _motionBlurEffect.Enabled = false
        end
        _motionBlurCurrentValue = 0
    end
    local row, setVisual = toggleRow(Utility, "Motion Blur", motionBlurEnabled, 15)
    _SV.setMotionBlurVisual = setVisual
    local _mblurBtn = row and row:FindFirstChild("ToggleButton")
    if _mblurBtn then
        _mblurBtn.Activated:Connect(function()
            motionBlurEnabled = not motionBlurEnabled
            if motionBlurEnabled then
                _startMotionBlur()
            else
                _stopMotionBlur()
            end
            if setVisual then setVisual(motionBlurEnabled) end
            saveAmbitiousConfig()
        end)
    end
    if motionBlurEnabled then
        _startMotionBlur()
    end

ambitiousGuiScaleValue = tonumber(savedConfig.ambitiousGuiScaleValue) or 0.75
ambitiousGuiScaleValue = math.clamp(tonumber(ambitiousGuiScaleValue) or 0.50, 0.50, 1.50)
ambitiousProgressBarScaleValue = tonumber(savedConfig.ambitiousProgressBarScaleValue) or 0.85
ambitiousMainScale = Main:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousMainScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")

ambitiousMainScale.Scale = ambitiousGuiScaleValue

local function applyAmbitiousProgressBarScale()
  local sg = HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubStealBarGui")
  local bar = sg and sg:FindFirstChild("StealBar")
  if not bar then return end
  local sc = bar:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousProgressBarScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")
  sc.Name = "AmbitiousProgressBarScale"
  sc.Scale = ambitiousProgressBarScaleValue
  sc.Parent = bar
  for _, d in ipairs(bar:GetDescendants()) do
    if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
      local base = d:GetAttribute("AmbBaseTextSize")
      if not base then
        base = d.TextSize
        d:SetAttribute("AmbBaseTextSize", base)
      end
      pcall(function()
        d.TextSize = math.max(1, math.floor(base * ambitiousProgressBarScaleValue + 0.5))
      end)
    end
  end
end
_G.AmbitiousApplyProgressBarScale = applyAmbitiousProgressBarScale

    for index, button in pairs(bgButtons) do
      local selected = (index == currentBackground) and (customBgSelected == nil)
      button.BackgroundTransparency = selected and 0.12 or 0.42
      local st = button:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

        st.Color = selected and Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255) or COLORS.strokeSoft

        st.Thickness = selected and 1.15 or 1

        local function tryConfirm()
          if isChecking then return end
          local raw = mBox.Text:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$")
          if raw == "" then mErr.Text = "Invalid Asset Please Try Again" return end
          local assetId = raw:match("^(%d+)$") or raw:match("rbxassetid://(%d+)$")
          if not assetId then mErr.Text = "Invalid Asset Please Try Again" return end
          for _, existId in ipairs(customBgIds) do
            if existId == assetId then mErr.Text = "Already added." return end
          end
          isChecking = true
          mErr.Text = ""
          mConfirm.Text = "CHECKING..."
          TweenService:Create(mConfirmHolder, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.55}):Play()
          task.spawn(function()
            local tempImg = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
            tempImg.Image = "rbxassetid://" .. assetId
            tempImg.Parent = miniOverlay
            local timeout = tick() + 4
            repeat task.wait(0.05) until tempImg.IsLoaded or tick() > timeout
            local valid = tempImg.IsLoaded
            tempImg:Destroy()
            if not miniOverlay or not miniOverlay.Parent then return end
            if valid then
              table.insert(customBgIds, assetId)
              saveAmbitiousConfig()
              if customBgPickerGui and customBgPickerGui.Parent then
                local sf = customBgPickerGui:FindFirstChild("Frame") and customBgPickerGui.Frame:FindFirstChild("Scroll")
                if sf then rebuildCustomThumbs(sf) end
              end
              miniOverlay:Destroy()
            else
              isChecking = false
              mErr.Text = "Invalid Asset Please Try Again"
              mConfirm.Text = "CONFIRM"
              TweenService:Create(mConfirmHolder, TweenInfo.new(0.12), {BackgroundTransparency = 0.28}):Play()
            end
          end)
        end
        mConfirm.MouseButton1Click:Connect(tryConfirm)
        mBox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enterPressed) if enterPressed then tryConfirm() end end)
        task.delay(0.05, function() if mBox and mBox.Parent then mBox:CaptureFocus() end end)
      end)

_G.AmbitiousStarbornFont = _G.AmbitiousStarbornFont
local _starbornFontReady = false
pcall(function()
  if type(isfile) ~= "function" or type(writefile) ~= "function"
     or type(getcustomasset) ~= "function" then
    return
  end
  if not isfile("starborn.ttf") then
    local ok, data = pcall(game.HttpGet, game, "https://files.catbox.moe/pwgdui.ttf")
    if not ok or not data or #data < 1000 then return end
    writefile("starborn.ttf", data)
  end
  if not isfile("starborn.json") then
    writefile("starborn.json", HttpService:JSONEncode({
      name = "Starborn",
      faces = {{
        name = "Regular",
        weight = 400,
        style = "normal",
        assetId = getcustomasset("starborn.ttf"),
      }}
    }))
  end
  _G.AmbitiousStarbornFont = Font.new(getcustomasset("starborn.json"))
  _starbornFontReady = _G.AmbitiousStarbornFont ~= nil
end)
_G.AmbitiousStarbornFontEnabled = _G.AmbitiousStarbornFontEnabled == true
_G.AmbitiousFonts = _G.AmbitiousFonts or {}
_G.AmbitiousFonts.orig = _G.AmbitiousFonts.orig or {}
_G.AmbitiousFonts.conns = _G.AmbitiousFonts.conns or {}
_G.AmbitiousFonts.size = _G.AmbitiousFonts.size or {}
_G.AmbitiousFontChoice = _G.AmbitiousFontChoice or "Default"
_G.AmbitiousFonts.WEIGHT_LABEL = {
  Thin = "Thin", ExtraLight = "ExLight", Light = "Light", Regular = "",
  Medium = "Medium", SemiBold = "SemiBold", Bold = "Bold",
  ExtraBold = "ExBold", Heavy = "Heavy",
}
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.list()
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  if F.cache then return F.cache end
  local out = {{ key = "Default", label = "Default", font = nil }}
  local seen, families = {}, {}
  for _, item in ipairs(Enum.Font:GetEnumItems()) do
    if item.Name ~= "Unknown" then
      local ok, f = pcall(function() return Font.fromEnum(item) end)
      if ok and f and f.Family and not seen[f.Family] then
        seen[f.Family] = true
        local nice = tostring(f.Family):match("([^/\\]+)%.json$")
                  or tostring(f.Family):match("([^/\\]+)$")
                  or item.Name
        families[#families + 1] = { family = f.Family, nice = nice }
      end
    end
  end
  for _, fam in ipairs(families) do
    for _, w in ipairs(Enum.FontWeight:GetEnumItems()) do
      for _, italic in ipairs({false, true}) do
        local ok, f = pcall(function()
          return Font.new(fam.family, w,
            italic and Enum.FontStyle.Italic or Enum.FontStyle.Normal)
        end)
        if ok and f then
          local wl = F.WEIGHT_LABEL[w.Name] or w.Name
          local label = fam.nice
          if wl ~= "" then label = label .. " " .. wl end
          if italic then label = label .. " Italic" end
          out[#out + 1] = {
            key = fam.family .. "|" .. w.Name .. "|" .. (italic and "i" or "n"),
            label = label,
            font = f,
          }
        end
      end
    end
  end
  F.cache = out
  return out
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.byKey(key)
  for _, e in ipairs(_G.AmbitiousFonts.list()) do
    if e.key == key then return e end
  end
  return nil
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.roots()
  local list = {}
  if HubGui then list[#list + 1] = HubGui end
  local char = LP.Character
  local head = char and char:FindFirstChild("Head")
  local oh = head and head:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubOverheadInfo")
  if oh then list[#list + 1] = oh end
  return list
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.isGlyph(obj)
  local t = obj.Text
  if t == nil then return false end
  if string.match(t, "%w") then return false end
  local ok, n = pcall(function() return utf8.len(t) end)
  return ok and n ~= nil and n <= 1
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.applyOne(obj, font)
  if not (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox")) then
    return
  end
  if obj:GetAttribute("AmbFontLock") then return end
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  if F.orig[obj] == nil then
    F.orig[obj] = obj.FontFace
    F.size[obj] = obj.TextSize
  end
  if F.isGlyph(obj) then font = nil end   -- i simboli restano l'originale
  pcall(function()
    obj.FontFace = font or F.orig[obj]
    if F.size[obj] then obj.TextSize = F.size[obj] end
  end)
end
_G.AmbitiousFonts.MIN_SIZE = 7    -- sotto e' illeggibile
_G.AmbitiousFonts.MARGIN   = 8    -- respiro ai lati, in pixel
_G.AmbitiousFonts.MAX_GROW = 1.35 -- quanto al massimo puo' crescere
_G.AmbitiousFonts.tconns = _G.AmbitiousFonts.tconns
  or setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
_G.AmbitiousFonts.lastLen = _G.AmbitiousFonts.lastLen
  or setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.params()
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  if F.gtbp == nil then
    local ok, p = pcall(function() return Instance.new("GetTextBoundsParams") end)
    F.gtbp = (ok and p) or false
  end
  return F.gtbp or nil
end
_G.AmbitiousFonts.m = _G.AmbitiousFonts.m or setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
_G.AmbitiousFonts.aconns = _G.AmbitiousFonts.aconns
  or setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
_G.AmbitiousFonts.stamp = _G.AmbitiousFonts.stamp or 0
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.reclamp(obj)
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  local c = F.m[obj]
  if not c then return end
  if F.isGlyph(obj) then
    if obj.TextSize ~= c.base then obj.TextSize = c.base end
    return
  end
  local s = c.s0
  local box = obj.AbsoluteSize
  if box.X > 1 and box.Y > 1 then
    local auto = obj.AutomaticSize
    local freeX = (auto == Enum.AutomaticSize.X or auto == Enum.AutomaticSize.XY)
    local freeY = (auto == Enum.AutomaticSize.Y or auto == Enum.AutomaticSize.XY)
    if not freeY then
      s = math.min(s, math.max(box.Y - 2, 1) / c.by)
    end
    if not (freeX or c.wrapped) then
      s = math.min(s, math.max(box.X - math.min(F.MARGIN, box.X * 0.08), 1) / c.bx)
    end
  end
  local want = math.floor(c.base * s + 0.5)
  if want < F.MIN_SIZE then want = F.MIN_SIZE end
  if obj.TextSize ~= want then obj.TextSize = want end
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.measure(obj)
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  local font = F.current()
  if not font then return false end
  local prm = F.params()
  if not prm then return false end
  F.tsvc = F.tsvc or game:GetService("TextService")
  local base = F.size[obj]
  if not base then base = obj.TextSize; F.size[obj] = base end
  local txt = obj.Text
  if txt == nil or txt == "" then
    F.m[obj] = {base = base, bx = 1, by = 1, s0 = 1, stamp = F.stamp, text = txt}
    return true
  end
  local wrapped = obj.TextWrapped and obj.AbsoluteSize.X > 1
  prm.Text = txt
  prm.Size = base
  prm.Width = wrapped and obj.AbsoluteSize.X or 0
  prm.Font = font
  local ok, b = pcall(function() return F.tsvc:GetTextBoundsAsync(prm) end)
  if not (ok and b and b.X > 0 and b.Y > 0) then return false end
  local s0 = 1
  local of = F.orig[obj]
  if of then
    prm.Font = of
    local ok2, b2 = pcall(function() return F.tsvc:GetTextBoundsAsync(prm) end)
    if ok2 and b2 and b2.X > 0 and b2.Y > 0 then
      s0 = wrapped and (b2.Y / b.Y) or (b2.X / b.X)
    end
  end
  if s0 > F.MAX_GROW then s0 = F.MAX_GROW end
  F.m[obj] = {base = base, bx = b.X, by = b.Y, s0 = s0,
              wrapped = wrapped, stamp = F.stamp, text = txt}
  return true
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.fitOne(obj)
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  if not F.current() then return end
  if obj:GetAttribute("AmbFontLock") then return end
  if not (obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox")) then return end
  if obj.TextScaled then return end
  if F.isGlyph(obj) then return true end
  local c = F.m[obj]
  if not (c and c.stamp == F.stamp and c.text == obj.Text) then
    if not F.measure(obj) then return false end
  end
  F.reclamp(obj)
  return true
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.watch(obj)
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  if F.aconns[obj] then return end
  F.aconns[obj] = obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteSize"):Connect(function()
    local G = _G.AmbitiousFonts
    if not G.current() then return end
    if G.m[obj] then G.reclamp(obj) else pcall(G.fitOne, obj) end
  end)
if F.tconns[obj] then return end
  F.tconns[obj] = obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
    local G = _G.AmbitiousFonts
    if not G.current() then return end
    if G.lastLen[obj] == #obj.Text then return end
    G.lastLen[obj] = #obj.Text
    pcall(G.applyOne, obj, G.current())
    pcall(G.fitOne, obj)
  end)
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.fit(pass)
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  F.fitToken = (F.fitToken or 0) + 1
  local myToken = F.fitToken
  if not F.current() then return end   -- Default: nessuna modifica
  F.tsvc = F.tsvc or game:GetService("TextService")
  task.spawn(function()
    local list = {}
    for _, root in ipairs(F.roots()) do
      for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if (d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox"))
           and not d.TextScaled and not d:GetAttribute("AmbFontLock") then
          list[#list + 1] = d
        end
      end
    end
    for _, o in ipairs(list) do
      if F.fitToken ~= myToken then return end   -- font cambiato nel frattempo
      if o.Parent then
        F.fitOne(o)
        F.lastLen[o] = #o.Text
        F.watch(o)
      end
    end
    pass = (tonumber(pass) or 0) + 1
    if pass < 3 then
      task.delay(pass == 1 and 0.35 or 1.5, function()
        if F.fitToken ~= myToken then return end
        F.stamp = F.stamp + 1
        F.fit(pass)
      end)
    end
  end)
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.current()
  if _G.AmbitiousStarbornFontEnabled == true and _G.AmbitiousStarbornFont then
    return _G.AmbitiousStarbornFont
  end
  local key = _G.AmbitiousFontChoice
  if not key or key == "Default" then return nil end
  local e = _G.AmbitiousFonts.byKey(key)
  return e and e.font or nil
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.applyAll()
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  local font = F.current()
  for _, root in ipairs(F.roots()) do
    F.applyOne(root, font)
    for _, d in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
      F.applyOne(d, font)
    end
  end
  F.fit()
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.hook()
  local F = _G.AmbitiousFonts
  for _, c in ipairs(F.conns) do pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end) end
  F.conns = {}
  for _, root in ipairs(F.roots()) do
    F.conns[#F.conns + 1] = root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
      if _G.AmbitiousStarbornFontEnabled ~= true and _G.AmbitiousFontChoice == "Default" then return end
      F.applyOne(d, F.current())
      pcall(F.fitOne, d)
      pcall(F.watch, d)
    end)
  end
end
function _G.AmbitiousFonts.set(key, skipSave)
  _G.AmbitiousFontChoice = key or "Default"
  _G.AmbitiousFonts.stamp = (_G.AmbitiousFonts.stamp or 0) + 1
  _G.AmbitiousFonts.applyAll()
  _G.AmbitiousFonts.hook()
  if not skipSave and saveAmbitiousConfig then pcall(saveAmbitiousConfig) end
end
function _G.AmbitiousSetStarbornFont(on, skipSave)
  on = on and true or false
  if on and not _starbornFontReady then
    _G.AmbitiousStarbornFontEnabled = false
    return false
  end
  _G.AmbitiousStarbornFontEnabled = on
  _G.AmbitiousFonts.stamp = (_G.AmbitiousFonts.stamp or 0) + 1
  _G.AmbitiousFonts.applyAll()
  _G.AmbitiousFonts.hook()
  if not skipSave and saveAmbitiousConfig then
    pcall(saveAmbitiousConfig)
  end
  return true
end
task.spawn(function() pcall(_G.AmbitiousFonts.list) end)
if not _G.AmbitiousFonts.charConn then
  _G.AmbitiousFonts.charConn = LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
    task.delay(1.5, function()
      if _G.AmbitiousStarbornFontEnabled ~= true and _G.AmbitiousFontChoice == "Default" then return end
      pcall(_G.AmbitiousFonts.applyAll)
      pcall(_G.AmbitiousFonts.hook)
    end)
  end)
end

      fpScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1

      fpScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 4

      fpScroll.ZIndex = 3
      fpScroll.Parent = fpFrame
      local FP_COLS, FP_CELL, FP_GAP = 3, 108, 10
      local FP_STEP = FP_CELL + FP_GAP
      local fpList = _G.AmbitiousFonts.list()
      local fpRows = math.ceil(#fpList / FP_COLS)
      fpScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 16 + fpRows * FP_STEP)
      local fpPool = {}
      local function fpEntryOf(card)
        local idx = card:GetAttribute("FontIdx")
        return idx and fpList[idx] or nil
      end
      local function fpPaint()
        for _, c in ipairs(fpPool) do
          local e = fpEntryOf(c)
          local sel = (e ~= nil and _G.AmbitiousFontChoice == e.key)
          local st = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
          if st then
            tween(st, {
              Color = sel and Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255) or Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 58),
              Transparency = sel and 0 or 0.55,
              Thickness = sel and 2.4 or 1.2,
            }, 0.12)
          end
        end
      end
      local function fpBind(card, idx)
        local e = fpList[idx]
        if not e then
          card:SetAttribute("FontIdx", nil)
          card.Visible = false
          return
        end
        local row = math.floor((idx - 1) / FP_COLS)
        local col = (idx - 1) % FP_COLS
        card.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10 + col * FP_STEP, 0, 8 + row * FP_STEP)
        card:SetAttribute("FontIdx", idx)
        local prv = card:FindFirstChild("Preview")
        if prv then
          if e.font then prv.FontFace = e.font else prv.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack end
        end
        local nl = card:FindFirstChild("FontName")
        if nl then nl.Text = e.label end
        card.Visible = true
      end
      for j = 1, (math.ceil(456 / FP_STEP) + 2) * FP_COLS do
        local card = Instance.new("TextButton")
        card.Name = "FontCard_" .. tostring(j)
        card.Size = UDim2.new(0, FP_CELL, 0, FP_CELL)
        card.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18)
        card.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        card.BorderSizePixel = 0

        card.Visible = false
        card.ZIndex = 4
        card.ClipsDescendants = true
        card.Parent = fpScroll
        corner(card, 18)
        local cardStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
        cardStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
        cardStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 58)
        cardStroke.Thickness = 1.2
        cardStroke.Transparency = 0.55
        cardStroke.Parent = card
        local prv = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        prv.Name = "Preview"
        prv.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        prv.Size = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0, 58)
        prv.Position = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 8)
        prv.Text = "Aa"
        prv.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        prv.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        prv.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
        prv.TextSize = 34
        prv.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        prv.ZIndex = 5
        prv.Parent = card
        prv:SetAttribute("AmbFontLock", true)
        local nameLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        nameLbl.Name = "FontName"
        nameLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 32)
        nameLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 1, -36)
        nameLbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        nameLbl.Text = ""
        nameLbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        nameLbl.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
        nameLbl.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
        nameLbl.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
        nameLbl.TextSize = 9
        nameLbl.TextWrapped = true
        nameLbl.ZIndex = 6
        nameLbl.Parent = card
        nameLbl:SetAttribute("AmbFontLock", true)
        card.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
          tween(cardStroke, {Transparency = 0, Thickness = 2.4}, 0.12)
          tween(prv, {TextSize = 40}, 0.12)
        end)
        card.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
          local e = fpEntryOf(card)
          local sel = (e ~= nil and _G.AmbitiousFontChoice == e.key)
          tween(cardStroke, {
            Transparency = sel and 0 or 0.55,
            Thickness = sel and 2.4 or 1.2,
          }, 0.12)
          tween(prv, {TextSize = 34}, 0.12)
        end)
        card.Activated:Connect(function()
          local e = fpEntryOf(card)
          if not e then return end
          _G.AmbitiousFonts.set(e.key)
          fpPaint()
        end)
        fpPool[#fpPool + 1] = card
      end
      local fpFirstRow = -1
      local function fpRender()
        local first = math.max(0, math.floor((fpScroll.CanvasPosition.Y - 8) / FP_STEP) - 1)
        if first == fpFirstRow then return end
        fpFirstRow = first
        for j, card in ipairs(fpPool) do
          fpBind(card, first * FP_COLS + j)
        end
        fpPaint()
      end
      fpScroll:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CanvasPosition"):Connect(fpRender)
      fpRender()
      fpBack.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
          closeFontPicker()
        end
      end)
      fpFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
      fpFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
      TweenService:Create(fpFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, 380, 0, 520),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -190, 0.5, -260)
      }):Play()
    end)
  end)()

    local function step(d)

      if _G.AmbitiousApplyTitleStyle then _G.AmbitiousApplyTitleStyle(idx) end

  function stepperRow(parent, labelText, defaultValue, order, callback, minValue, maxValue)
    local row = Instance.new("Frame")
    row.Name = labelText
    row.BackgroundColor3 = COLORS.row
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.22
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 42)
    row.BorderSizePixel = 0

    row.Parent = parent
    corner(row, 10)
    stroke(row, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1.15, 0.32)
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Name = "Label"
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = labelText
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
    label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.25
    label.TextSize = 11
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamMedium
    label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -155, 1, 0)
    label.ZIndex = 5
    label.Parent = row
    local value = defaultValue
    local minus = Instance.new("TextButton")
    minus.Name = "Minus"
    minus.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
    minus.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    minus.BorderSizePixel = 0
    minus.Text = "-"
    minus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
    minus.TextSize = 14
    minus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    minus.AutoButtonColor = false
    minus.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 26)
    minus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -118, 0.5, -13)
    minus.ZIndex = 6
    minus.Parent = row
    corner(minus, 7)
    stroke(minus, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.5)
    local valueBox = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    valueBox.Name = "Value"
    valueBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
    valueBox.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
    valueBox.BorderSizePixel = 0
    valueBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", value)
    valueBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
    valueBox.TextSize = 13
    valueBox.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    valueBox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
    valueBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 26)
    valueBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0.5, -13)
    valueBox.ZIndex = 6
    valueBox.Parent = row
    corner(valueBox, 7)
    stroke(valueBox, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.5)
    local plus = Instance.new("TextButton")
    plus.Name = "Plus"
    plus.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
    plus.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    plus.BorderSizePixel = 0
    plus.Text = "+"
    plus.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 245, 255)
    plus.TextSize = 14
    plus.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    plus.AutoButtonColor = false
    plus.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 26)
    plus.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0.5, -13)
    plus.ZIndex = 6
    plus.Parent = row
    corner(plus, 7)
    stroke(plus, COLORS.strokeSoft, 1, 0.5)
    local function setValue(nextValue)
      value = math.clamp(math.floor((nextValue * 100) + 0.5) / 100, minValue or 0.50, maxValue or 1.50)
      valueBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", value)
      if callback then callback(value) end
    end
    minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
      setValue(value - 0.05)
    end)
    plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
      setValue(value + 0.05)
    end)
    return row
  end

    local mpGui = nil
    local function closeMusicPlayer()
      _G.AmbitiousMusicRefreshUI = nil
      if mpGui and mpGui.Parent then
        local fr = mpGui:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        if fr then TweenService:Create(fr, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {BackgroundTransparency = 1}):Play() end
        local g = mpGui
        mpGui = nil
        task.delay(0.25, function()
          pcall(function() if g and g.Parent then g:Destroy() end end)
        end)
      else
        mpGui = nil
      end
    end
    _G.AmbitiousMusicCloseUI = closeMusicPlayer
    local function _mpFmtTime(t)
      t = math.max(0, math.floor(tonumber(t) or 0))
      return string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(t / 60), t % 60)
    end
    local function _mpSmallBtn(parent, name, text, size, pos, textSize)
      local b = Instance.new("TextButton")

      b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 14)
      b.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
      b.BorderSizePixel = 0
      b.Text = text

      b.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
      b.AutoButtonColor = false

      b.Position = pos
      b.ZIndex = 6
      b.Parent = parent
      corner(b, 10)
      stroke(b, Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 105), 1, 0.5)
      b.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(b, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 40)}, 0.1) end)
      b.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(b, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 14)}, 0.1) end)
      return b
    end

    local pill = Instance.new("TextButton")
    pill.Name = "Pill"
    pill.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    pill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    pill.Text = "OFF"
    pill.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
    pill.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    pill.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.35
    pill.TextSize = 11
    pill.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    pill.AutoButtonColor = false
    pill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 24)
    pill.Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0.5, -12)
    pill.ZIndex = 7
    pill.Parent = f
    corner(pill, 12)
    local function apply(v)
        local isGrey = _G._AmbitiousAccentIsGrey and _G._AmbitiousAccentIsGrey() or false
        local onBase, onEdge = Color3.fromRGB(147, 51, 234), Color3.fromRGB(216, 180, 254)
        if _G._AmbitiousToggleOnColor then
            local b, e = _G._AmbitiousToggleOnColor()
            if b then onBase = b end
            if e then onEdge = e end
        end
        if v then
            pill.Text = "ON"
            pill.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)   -- BIANCO
            pillBg.BackgroundColor3 = onBase
            pillBgGrad.Enabled = true   -- SEMPRE ATTIVO, ma il gradiente avrà colori appropriati (viola o grigio) grazie al registrazione
            pillBgStroke.Color = onEdge
            pillBgStroke.Transparency = 0.15
        else
            pill.Text = "OFF"
            pill.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 180)
            pillBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 14)
            pillBgGrad.Enabled = false
            pillBgStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(90, 90, 105)
            pillBgStroke.Transparency = 0.5
        end
    end
    apply(getVal() == true)
    pill.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        local nv = not (getVal() == true)
        setVal(nv)
        apply(nv)
    end)
    pill.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
        tween(pillBg, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 66, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.new(1, -75, 0.5, -14)}, 0.12)
        tween(pill,   {Size = UDim2.new(0, 66, 0, 28), Position = UDim2.new(1, -75, 0.5, -14)}, 0.12)
    end)
    pill.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
        tween(pillBg, {Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0.5, -12)}, 0.12)
        tween(pill,   {Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 24), Position = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0.5, -12)}, 0.12)
    end)
    return f, apply
end

        spdValue.Text = string.format("%.1fx", musicPlayerSpeed)

        setMusicSpeed(musicPlayerSpeed + 0.1)
        spdValue.Text = string.format("%.1fx", musicPlayerSpeed)
      end)
      local function refresh()
        if not (mpGui and mpGui.Parent) then return end
        nameLbl.Text = getMusicSongName()
        counterLbl.Text = "TRACK " .. tostring(selectedMusicSong) .. " / " .. tostring(#MUSIC_PLAYER_OPTIONS)
        volValue.Text = string.format("%.2f", musicPlayerVolume)
        spdValue.Text = string.format("%.1fx", musicPlayerSpeed)
        if applyAutoNext then applyAutoNext(musicAutoPlayNext == true) end
        if applyShuffle then applyShuffle(musicShuffleEnabled == true) end
        if applyLoop then applyLoop(musicLoopEnabled == true) end
        local s = _musicPlayerSound
        local alive = s and s.Parent
        playBtn.Text = (alive and s.IsPlaying) and "PAUSE" or "PLAY"
        if alive then
          local sp = tonumber(musicPlayerSpeed) or 1
          if sp <= 0 then sp = 1 end
          local len = (s.TimeLength or 0) / sp
          local pos = (s.TimePosition or 0) / sp
          if len > 0 then
            barFill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(pos / len, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
            curLbl.Text = _mpFmtTime(pos)
            totLbl.Text = _mpFmtTime(len)
          else
            barFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
            curLbl.Text = "0:00"
            totLbl.Text = "--:--"
          end
        else
          barFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
          curLbl.Text = "0:00"
          totLbl.Text = "0:00"
        end
      end
      _G.AmbitiousMusicRefreshUI = refresh
      refresh()
      task.spawn(function()
        while mpGui and mpGui.Parent do
          pcall(refresh)
          task.wait(0.2)
        end
      end)
      backdrop.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
        if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
          closeMusicPlayer()
        end
      end)
      frame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
      frame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
      TweenService:Create(frame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Size = UDim2.new(0, POPUP_W, 0, POPUP_H),
        Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -POPUP_W / 2, 0.5, -POPUP_H / 2)
      }):Play()
    end)
  end
