      if _G.AmbitiousResetStealBarPosition then pcall(_G.AmbitiousResetStealBarPosition) end
      if _G.AmbitiousResetMobileButtons then _G.AmbitiousResetMobileButtons() end
      if _G.AmbitiousRefreshStretchSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshStretchSelector() end
      if _G.AmbitiousDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates then _G.AmbitiousDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates() end
      if _G.AmbitiousStopMedCounter then _G.AmbitiousStopMedCounter() end
      if _G.AmbitiousRefreshInfJumpModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshInfJumpModeSelector() end
      if _G.AmbitiousRefreshAntiRagdollModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshAntiRagdollModeSelector() end
      if _G.AmbitiousRefreshAutoCarryModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshAutoCarryModeSelector() end
      if _G.AmbitiousRefreshBatCounterModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshBatCounterModeSelector() end
      if _SV.setLookAtVisual then pcall(function() _SV.setLookAtVisual(false) end) end
      if _G.AmbitiousLookAtRadiusBox then _G.AmbitiousLookAtRadiusBox.Text = "20" end
      if _G.AmbitiousSetRainbowJoystick then
        pcall(_G.AmbitiousSetRainbowJoystick, false)
      end
      _G.AmbitiousRainbowJoystickEnabled = false
      if _G._AmbitiousSyncAfterHit and _G._AmbitiousSyncAfterHit.setVisual then
        pcall(_G._AmbitiousSyncAfterHit.setVisual, false)
      end
      for _, _vn in ipairs({
        "setAntiDieVisual",
        "setAntiLagVisual",
        "setAutoTPVisual",
        "setFOVVisual",
        "setNoCamCollisionVisual",
        "setPlayerESPVisual",
        "setRagdollCountdownVisual",
        "setTrackerESPVisual",
        "setUnwalkVisual",
        "setRainbowJoystickVisual",
      }) do
        local _fn = _SV[_vn]
        if type(_fn) == "function" then pcall(_fn, false) end
      end
      saveAmbitiousConfig()
    end)
    task.wait(0.35)
    setResetDoneTheme()
    task.wait(0.6)
    pcall(function()
      local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
      TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, Players.LocalPlayer)
    end)
  end)
end
task.wait()
__AmbitiousDuelsSetupVisualsUI()
_G.__AmbitiousDuelsSetupSettingsUI()
if AmbitiousUpdateGuiLockVisual then AmbitiousUpdateGuiLockVisual() end
if _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonsHidden then _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonsHidden() end
UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
  local isControllerInput = tostring(input.UserInputType):find("Gamepad") ~= nil
  local isMB3 = input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3
  local function isKeyMatch(boundKey)
    if not boundKey then return false end
    if boundKey == "MB3" then return isMB3 end
    return (not isMB3) and input.KeyCode == boundKey
  end
  if gameProcessed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and not listeningForSpeedKey and not listeningForTPDownKey then return end
  if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
  if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and not isControllerInput and not isMB3 then return end
  if not isMB3 and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.ToggleUI) then
    if Main.Visible then
      if _G.AmbitiousHubClose then _G.AmbitiousHubClose(true)
      else Main.Visible = false; MiniFrame.Visible = true end
    else
      if _G.AmbitiousHubOpen then _G.AmbitiousHubOpen()
      else Main.Visible = true; MiniFrame.Visible = false; Main.Size = FULL_MAIN_SIZE end
    end
    saveAmbitiousConfig()
    return
  end
  if listeningForSpeedKey then
    if tick() - (keybindListenStartedAt or 0) < 0.18 then return end
    local targetKey = listeningForSpeedKey
    if not isMB3 and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
      listeningForSpeedKey = nil
      refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
      return
    end
    if not isMB3 and (input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Delete) then
      speedKeybinds[targetKey] = nil
    else
      local pressedKey = isMB3 and "MB3" or input.KeyCode
      for otherKeyId, boundKey in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
        if otherKeyId ~= targetKey and boundKey == pressedKey then
          speedKeybinds[otherKeyId] = nil
        end
      end
      if tpDownKeybind == pressedKey then
        tpDownKeybind = nil
        refreshTPDownKeybind()
      end
      speedKeybinds[targetKey] = pressedKey
    end
    listeningForSpeedKey = nil
    refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
    saveAmbitiousConfig()
    return
  end
  if listeningForTPDownKey then
    if tick() - (keybindListenStartedAt or 0) < 0.18 then return end
    if not isMB3 and input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
      listeningForTPDownKey = false
      refreshTPDownKeybind()
      return
    end
    if not isMB3 and (input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Delete) then
      tpDownKeybind = nil
    else
      local pressedKey = isMB3 and "MB3" or input.KeyCode
      for keyId, boundKey in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
        if boundKey == pressedKey then
          speedKeybinds[keyId] = nil
        end
      end
      tpDownKeybind = pressedKey
    end
    listeningForTPDownKey = false
    refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
    refreshTPDownKeybind()
    saveAmbitiousConfig()
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.SpeedToggle) then
    toggleCarryMode()
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.LaggerToggle) then
    toggleLaggerMode()
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.Aimbot) then
    if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked and _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked() then
      if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeForceStop then _G.AmbitiousSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
      return
    end
    if _G.AmbitiousToggleSelectedAimbot then
      _G.AmbitiousToggleSelectedAimbot()
    elseif selectedAimbotMode == "V2" and _G.AmbitiousStartBatAimbotV2 and _G.AmbitiousStopBatAimbotV2 then
      if _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2On then _G.AmbitiousStopBatAimbotV2() else _G.AmbitiousStartBatAimbotV2() end
    elseif _G.AmbitiousStartNormalAimbot and _G.AmbitiousStopNormalAimbot then
      if _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotOn then _G.AmbitiousStopNormalAimbot() else _G.AmbitiousStartNormalAimbot() end
    end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.AmbitiousRefreshAimbotVisual() end
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.TPBat) then
    if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked and _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked() then
      if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeForceStop then _G.AmbitiousSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
      return
    end
    if _G.AmbitiousToggleTPBatManual then
      _G.AmbitiousToggleTPBatManual()
    elseif _G.AmbitiousToggleTPBat then
      _G.AmbitiousToggleTPBat()
    end
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.DropBrainrot) then
    if selectedDropMode == "Stand Still" then
      runDropStandStill()
    else
      runDropBrainrot()
    end
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.AutoPlay) then
    if _G.AmbitiousAutoPlayToggle then _G.AmbitiousAutoPlayToggle() end
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.AntiTPBat) then
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBat then
      _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBat.toggle()
    elseif _G.AmbitiousToggleAntiTPBat then
      _G.AmbitiousToggleAntiTPBat()
    end
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.Float) then
    if _G.AmbitiousFloatToggle then _G.AmbitiousFloatToggle() end
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(speedKeybinds.InstantReset) then
    if _G.AmbitiousInstantReset then _G.AmbitiousInstantReset() end
    return
  end
  if isKeyMatch(tpDownKeybind) then
    runTPFloor()
    return
  end
end)
setTab("MOVEMENT")
_G.__AmbitiousDuelsSetupStealBar = function()
  local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
  local UIS = UserInputService
  local TS = TweenService
  local existingStealBar = HubGui and HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubStealBarGui")
  if existingStealBar then existingStealBar:Destroy() end
  existingStealBar = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubStealBarGui")
  if existingStealBar then existingStealBar:Destroy() end
  local W, H = 380, 44
  local RADIUS = 11
  local C = {
    line   = Color3.fromRGB(140, 90, 235),
    txt    = Color3.fromRGB(246, 243, 255),
    txtDim = Color3.fromRGB(150, 143, 168),
    good   = Color3.fromRGB(130, 245, 160),
    warn   = Color3.fromRGB(255, 214, 100),
    bad    = Color3.fromRGB(255, 105, 105),
  }
  local GRAD_ACTIVE = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB( 88,  40, 210)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, Color3.fromRGB(150,  70, 245)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.78, Color3.fromRGB(224, 100, 255)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 168, 240)),
  })
  local GRAD_IDLE = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB( 60,  56,  78)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(108, 100, 132)),
  })
  local GRAD_DONE = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB( 30, 150, 130)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.55, Color3.fromRGB( 80, 220, 170)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(170, 255, 205)),
  })
  local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
  gui.Name = "AmbitiousHubStealBarGui"
  gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
  gui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
  gui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
  gui.DisplayOrder = 50
  gui.Parent = HubGui
  function drag(frame)
    local dragging, dragStart, startPos = false, nil, nil
    frame.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
      if _G.AmbitiousGuiLocked == true then return end
      if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
        dragging = true
        dragStart = input.Position
        startPos = frame.Position
        input.Changed:Connect(function()
          if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
            dragging = false
            if frame and frame.Name == "StealBar" then
              savedStealBarPositionTable = udim2ToTable(frame.Position)
              pcall(saveAmbitiousConfig)
            end
          end
        end)
      end
    end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
      if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
        local delta = input.Position - dragStart
        frame.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + delta.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + delta.Y)
      end
    end)
  end
  local pbFrame = Instance.new("Frame", gui)
  pbFrame.Name = "StealBar"
  pbFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, W, 0, H)
  _G.AmbitiousStealBarDefaultPosition = UDim2.new(0.5, -math.floor(W / 2), 1, -128)
  pbFrame.Position = tableToUDim2(savedStealBarPositionTable, _G.AmbitiousStealBarDefaultPosition)
  savedStealBarPositionTable = udim2ToTable(pbFrame.Position)
  pbFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
  pbFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
  pbFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
  pbFrame.Active = true
  pbFrame.ClipsDescendants = true
  pbFrame.ZIndex = 2
  Instance.new("UICorner", pbFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, RADIUS)
  local cardStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", pbFrame)
  cardStroke.Color = C.line
  cardStroke.Thickness = 1.2
  cardStroke.Transparency = 0.4
  drag(pbFrame)
  pbFrame:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Position"):Connect(function()
    savedStealBarPositionTable = udim2ToTable(pbFrame.Position)
  end)
  _G.AmbitiousResetStealBarPosition = function()
    if pbFrame and pbFrame.Parent then
      pbFrame.Position = _G.AmbitiousStealBarDefaultPosition or UDim2.new(0.5, -176, 1, -128)
      savedStealBarPositionTable = udim2ToTable(pbFrame.Position)
    else
      savedStealBarPositionTable = nil
    end
  end
  _G.AmbitiousApplyStealBarPosition = function()
    if pbFrame and pbFrame.Parent then
      pbFrame.Position = tableToUDim2(savedStealBarPositionTable, _G.AmbitiousStealBarDefaultPosition or UDim2.new(0.5, -176, 1, -128))
      savedStealBarPositionTable = udim2ToTable(pbFrame.Position)
    end
  end
  local pbScale = Instance.new("UIScale")
  pbScale.Name = "AmbitiousProgressBarScale"
  pbScale.Scale = ambitiousProgressBarScaleValue or 1
  pbScale.Parent = pbFrame
  task.defer(function()
    if _G.AmbitiousApplyProgressBarScale then pcall(_G.AmbitiousApplyProgressBarScale) end
  end)
  pbFrame.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(d)
    if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") or d:IsA("TextBox") then
      task.defer(function()
        if _G.AmbitiousApplyProgressBarScale then pcall(_G.AmbitiousApplyProgressBarScale) end
      end)
    end
  end)
  local bgImage = Instance.new("ImageLabel", pbFrame)
  bgImage.Name = "BarBackground"
  bgImage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
  bgImage.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
  bgImage.BackgroundTransparency = 1

    local hub = HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHub")

    return main and main:FindFirstChild("CustomBackground") or nil
  end
  local bgSrcConn = nil
  local function syncBarBackground()
    local src = hubBgSource()
    if src and src.Image and src.Image ~= "" then
      bgImage.Image = src.Image
      bgImage.Visible = true
      if not bgSrcConn then
        bgSrcConn = src:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Image"):Connect(function()
          if src.Image ~= "" then
            bgImage.Image = src.Image
            bgImage.Visible = true
          else
            bgImage.Visible = false
          end
        end)
      end
      return true
    end
    bgImage.Visible = false
    return false
  end
  _G.AmbitiousStealBarSyncBackground = syncBarBackground

  local washClip = Instance.new("Frame", pbFrame)

  local sbFpsLabel = statRow(1, "FPS")

  statDiv.ZIndex = 10
  local sbPingLabel = statRow(3, "MS")
  sbPingLabel.Name = "SBPing"
  local progressRadLbl = Instance.new("TextLabel", pbFrame)
  progressRadLbl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
  progressRadLbl.Visible = false
  local barState = "IDLE"
  local targetP, shownP = 0, 0
  function setBarState(state)
    state = tostring(state or "IDLE"):upper()
    barState = state
    local active = (state ~= "IDLE")
    local done = (state == "DONE" or state == "SUCCESS" or state == "STOLEN")
    stealLbl.Text = (state == "IDLE") and "STEAL" or state
    washGrad.Color = done and GRAD_DONE or (active and GRAD_ACTIVE or GRAD_IDLE)
    local col = active and C.txt or C.txtDim
    TS:Create(stealLbl,    TweenInfo.new(0.18), {TextColor3 = col}):Play()
    TS:Create(progressPct, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {TextColor3 = col}):Play()
    TS:Create(cardStroke,  TweenInfo.new(0.22), {Transparency = active and 0.1 or 0.45}):Play()
  end
  local renderConn
  renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    if not pbFrame.Parent then
      pcall(function() renderConn:Disconnect() end)
      return
    end
    if shownP ~= targetP then
      local a = 1 - math.exp(-38 * math.min(dt, 0.1))
      shownP = shownP + (targetP - shownP) * a
      if math.abs(targetP - shownP) < 0.004 then shownP = targetP end
    end
    washClip.Size = UDim2.new(shownP, 0, 1, 0)
    progressPct.Text = math.floor(shownP * 100 + 0.5) .. "%"
    if shownP > 0.05 and shownP < 0.94 and targetP > 0.05 and targetP < 0.999 and barState ~= "IDLE" then
      edge.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
      edge.Position = UDim2.new(shownP, 0, 0, 0)
    else
      edge.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    end
  end)
  local StealBar = {}
  function StealBar.SetProgress(p)
    p = math.clamp(tonumber(p) or 0, 0, 1)
    targetP = p
    if p >= 0.999 then
      shownP = 1
      washClip.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
      edge.BackgroundTransparency = 1
      progressPct.Text = "100%"
    end
  end
  function StealBar.Reset()
    targetP = 0
    shownP = 0
    washClip.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    wash.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    edge.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    edge.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    progressPct.Text = "0%"
    setBarState("IDLE")
  end
  function StealBar.SetState(state)
    setBarState(state)
  end
  setBarState("IDLE")
  task.spawn(function()
    local sbFpsLastTime = tick()
    local sbFpsFrameCount = 0
    local sbFpsCurrent = 0
    local sbPingCurrent = 0
    local sbLastPingUpdate = tick()
    local sbFpsConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
      sbFpsFrameCount = sbFpsFrameCount + 1
      local now = tick()
      local elapsed = now - sbFpsLastTime
      if elapsed >= 0.25 then
        sbFpsCurrent = math.floor(sbFpsFrameCount / elapsed + 0.5)
        sbFpsFrameCount = 0
        sbFpsLastTime = now
      end
    end)
    while pbFrame and pbFrame.Parent do
      local now = tick()
      if now - sbLastPingUpdate >= 0.5 then
        sbLastPingUpdate = now
        pcall(function()
          local rawPing = LP:GetNetworkPing()
          if rawPing then sbPingCurrent = math.floor(rawPing * 1000 + 0.5) end
        end)
      end
      sbFpsLabel.Text = tostring(sbFpsCurrent)
      sbPingLabel.Text = tostring(sbPingCurrent)
      if sbFpsCurrent >= 120 then
        sbFpsLabel.TextColor3 = C.good
      elseif sbFpsCurrent >= 60 then
        sbFpsLabel.TextColor3 = C.warn
      else
        sbFpsLabel.TextColor3 = C.bad
      end
      if sbPingCurrent <= 50 then
        sbPingLabel.TextColor3 = C.good
      elseif sbPingCurrent <= 100 then
        sbPingLabel.TextColor3 = C.warn
      else
        sbPingLabel.TextColor3 = C.bad
      end
      pcall(syncBarBackground)
      local kwBusy = (_G._AmbitiousKickWarn and _G._AmbitiousKickWarn.active == true)
        or (HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubKickWarning") ~= nil)
      if highPingWarningEnabled and not kwBusy and sbPingCurrent > 120 and tick() >= (_G._AmbitiousHPWNextAllowed or 0) then
        local existingWarning = HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubPingWarning")
        if not existingWarning then
          local warningGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
          warningGui.Name = "AmbitiousHubPingWarning"
          warningGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
          warningGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
          warningGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
          warningGui.DisplayOrder = 99999
          warningGui.Parent = HubGui
          _G._AmbitiousHPWNextAllowed = tick() + 13.5
          local warningFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
          warningFrame.Name = "WarningFrame"
          warningFrame.Size = UDim2.new(0, 420, 0, 52)
          warningFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -210, 0, -60)
          warningFrame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 30, 30)
          warningFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.12
          warningFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
          warningFrame.ZIndex = 1000
          warningFrame.Parent = warningGui
          Instance.new("UICorner", warningFrame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 12)
          local warningStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", warningFrame)
          warningStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 80, 80)
          warningStroke.Thickness = 2
          warningStroke.Transparency = 0.15
          warningStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
          local warningIcon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
          warningIcon.Name = "WarningIcon"
          warningIcon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 1, 0)
          warningIcon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
          warningIcon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
          warningIcon.Text = "⚠️"
          warningIcon.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
          warningIcon.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
          warningIcon.TextSize = 26
          warningIcon.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
          warningIcon.ZIndex = 1001
          warningIcon.Parent = warningFrame
          local warningText = Instance.new("TextLabel")
          warningText.Name = "WarningText"
          warningText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -56, 1, 0)
          warningText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 0)
          warningText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
          warningText.Text = "MORE THAN 120 PING DONT DUEL"
          warningText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
          warningText.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
          warningText.TextSize = 16
          warningText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
          warningText.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
          warningText.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.25
          warningText.ZIndex = 1001
          warningText.Parent = warningFrame
          TweenService:Create(warningFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
            Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -210, 0, 18)
          }):Play()
          task.delay(3, function()
            if warningGui and warningGui.Parent then
              TweenService:Create(warningFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
                Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -210, 0, -60),
                BackgroundTransparency = 1
              }):Play()
              TweenService:Create(warningText, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextTransparency = 1}):Play()
              TweenService:Create(warningIcon, TweenInfo.new(0.2), {TextTransparency = 1}):Play()
              task.wait(0.35)
              pcall(function() warningGui:Destroy() end)
              _G._AmbitiousHPWNextAllowed = tick() + 10
            end
          end)
        end
      end
      task.wait(0.5)
    end
    pcall(function() sbFpsConn:Disconnect() end)
    pcall(function() renderConn:Disconnect() end)
    pcall(function() if bgSrcConn then bgSrcConn:Disconnect() end end)
  end)
  _G.StealBar = StealBar
end
_G.__AmbitiousDuelsSetupStealBar()

_G.__AmbitiousDuelsSetupMinimizeToggle = function()
  _G.__AmbitiousDuelsMinimized = false
  Close.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if Main.Visible then
      if _G.AmbitiousHubClose then _G.AmbitiousHubClose(true)
      else _G.__AmbitiousDuelsMinimized = true; Main.Visible = false; MiniFrame.Visible = true end
    else
      if _G.AmbitiousHubOpen then _G.AmbitiousHubOpen()
      else _G.__AmbitiousDuelsMinimized = false; Main.Visible = true; MiniFrame.Visible = false; Main.Size = FULL_MAIN_SIZE end
    end
    saveAmbitiousConfig()
  end)
end

_G.__AmbitiousDuelsRunIntro = function()
  local TS = TweenService
  local introGuiParent = Gui and Gui.Parent or HubGui
  local origSize = FULL_MAIN_SIZE or Main.Size
  local wasMinimizedBeforeIntro = (_G.__AmbitiousDuelsMinimized == true)
  if not _introEnabled then
    stopIntroPlayback()
    stopIntroPreview()
    Main.Size = origSize
    if not wasMinimizedBeforeIntro then
      Main.Visible = true
      MiniFrame.Visible = false
    else
      Main.Visible = false
      MiniFrame.Visible = true
    end
    return
  end
  playIntroMusic()
  Main.Visible = false
  MiniFrame.Visible = false
  Main.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
  task.spawn(function()
    local introGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
    introGui.Name = "AmbitiousHubIntro"
    introGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
    introGui.DisplayOrder = 100
    introGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
    introGui.Parent = introGuiParent
    local introActive = true
    function finishIntro()
      if not introActive then return end
      introActive = false
      stopIntroPlayback()
      MiniFrame.Visible = false
      Main.Visible = true
      pcall(function()
        TS:Create(Main, TweenInfo.new(0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Back, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Size = origSize}):Play()
      end)
      task.delay(0.75, function()
        pcall(function() introGui:Destroy() end)
      end)
    end
    local darkBg = Instance.new("Frame", introGui)
    darkBg.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    darkBg.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 10)
    darkBg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    darkBg.BorderSizePixel = 0
    darkBg.ZIndex = 1
    local bgGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", darkBg)
    bgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
      ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(42,42,46)),
      ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.45, Color3.fromRGB(18,18,20)),
      ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(10,10,12))
    })
    bgGrad.Rotation = 90
    local redWash = Instance.new("Frame", introGui)
    redWash.Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
    redWash.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    redWash.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    redWash.BorderSizePixel = 0
    redWash.ZIndex = 2
local skipBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", introGui)
skipBtn.Name = "SkipIntro"

local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient", skipBtn)
grad.Rotation = 0
grad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(220, 100, 255)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, Color3.fromRGB(120, 60, 220)),
    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(40, 20, 150)),
})
local function shiftHue(color, offset)
    local r, g, b = color.R, color.G, color.B
    local max = math.max(r, g, b)
    local min = math.min(r, g, b)
    local h, s, v
    v = max
    local d = max - min
    s = (max == 0) and 0 or d / max
    if max == min then
        h = 0
    else
        if max == r then
            h = (g - b) / d + (g < b and 6 or 0)
        elseif max == g then
            h = (b - r) / d + 2
        else
            h = (r - g) / d + 4
        end
        h = h / 6
    end
    h = (h + offset) % 1
    local function hueToRgb(p, q, t)
        if t < 0 then t = t + 1 end
        if t > 1 then t = t - 1 end
        if t < 1/6 then return p + (q - p) * 6 * t end
        if t < 1/2 then return q end
        if t < 2/3 then return p + (q - p) * (2/3 - t) * 6 end
        return p
    end
    if s == 0 then
        return Color3.fromRGB(v, v, v)
    end
    local q = v * (1 - s * (1 - (h * 6 % 1)))
    local p = v * (1 - s)
    local tr = v
    local tg = v
    local tb = v
    if h < 1/6 then
        tr = v
        tg = v * (1 - s * (1 - (h * 6)))
        tb = v * (1 - s)
    elseif h < 1/3 then
        tr = v * (1 - s * (h * 6 - 1))
        tg = v
        tb = v * (1 - s)
    elseif h < 1/2 then
        tr = v * (1 - s)
        tg = v
        tb = v * (1 - s * (1 - (h * 6 - 2)))
    elseif h < 2/3 then
        tr = v * (1 - s)
        tg = v * (1 - s * (h * 6 - 3))
        tb = v
    elseif h < 5/6 then
        tr = v * (1 - s * (1 - (h * 6 - 4)))
        tg = v * (1 - s)
        tb = v
    else
        tr = v
        tg = v * (1 - s)
        tb = v * (1 - s * (h * 6 - 5))
    end
    return Color3.new(tr, tg, tb)
end
task.spawn(function()
    local hueOffset = 0
    local speed = 0.002  -- velocità lenta per effetto morbido
    while skipBtn and skipBtn.Parent do
        hueOffset = (hueOffset + speed) % 1
        local c1 = shiftHue(Color3.fromRGB(220, 100, 255), hueOffset)
        local c2 = shiftHue(Color3.fromRGB(120, 60, 220), hueOffset)
        local c3 = shiftHue(Color3.fromRGB(40, 20, 150), hueOffset)
        grad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, c1),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, c2),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, c3),
        })
        task.wait(0.05)
    end
end)

_G.AmbitiousDuelsForceSyncLoadedButtons = function()
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setAutoTPVisual then _SV.setAutoTPVisual(autoTPEnabled) end
    if autoTPEnabled then startAutoTP() else stopAutoTP() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setInfJumpVisual then setInfJumpVisual(infJumpEnabled) end
    if setInfJumpInternal then setInfJumpInternal(infJumpEnabled) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshInfJumpModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshInfJumpModeSelector() end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshTPDownMethodSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshTPDownMethodSelector() end   -- >>> TP DOWN METHOD <<<
    if _G.AmbitiousFonts then
      pcall(_G.AmbitiousFonts.applyAll)
      pcall(_G.AmbitiousFonts.hook)
    end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setLookAtVisual then _SV.setLookAtVisual(_G.AmbitiousLookAtEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousLookAtRadiusBox then _G.AmbitiousLookAtRadiusBox.Text = tostring(tonumber(_G.AmbitiousLookAtRadius) or 20) end
    if _G.AmbitiousLookAtEnabled then
      if _G.AmbitiousStartLookAt then _G.AmbitiousStartLookAt() end
    else
      if _G.AmbitiousStopLookAt then _G.AmbitiousStopLookAt() end
    end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setAutoCarrySpeedVisual then setAutoCarrySpeedVisual(autoCarrySpeedEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshAutoCarryModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshAutoCarryModeSelector() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setAntiRagdollVisual then setAntiRagdollVisual(antiRagdollEnabled) end
    if _SV.setTPRagdollVisual then _SV.setTPRagdollVisual(_G.AmbitiousTPRagdollEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshAntiRagdollModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshAntiRagdollModeSelector() end
    setAntiRagdoll(antiRagdollEnabled)
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setAntiDieVisual then _SV.setAntiDieVisual(_G._AmbitiousAntiDieUserWanted == true) end
    setAntiDie(_G._AmbitiousAntiDieUserWanted == true)
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiDieAutoSync then _G.AmbitiousAntiDieAutoSync() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _G.AmbitiousSetAutoLeft then _G.AmbitiousSetAutoLeft(autoLeftEnabled, true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousSetAutoRight then _G.AmbitiousSetAutoRight(autoRightEnabled, true) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setAutoStealVisual then setAutoStealVisual(autoStealEnabled) end
    if _G.AmbitiousAutoStealSync then _G.AmbitiousAutoStealSync() end
    if _G.AmbitiousSetRadiusCircle then _G.AmbitiousSetRadiusCircle(_G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleSetVisual then _G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleSetVisual(_G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousSetRadiusCircleColor then _G.AmbitiousSetRadiusCircleColor(_G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleColor) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleSetColorPreview then _G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleSetColorPreview(_G.AmbitiousRadiusCircleColor) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _G.AmbitiousNormalAutoSwingSetVisual then _G.AmbitiousNormalAutoSwingSetVisual(autoSwingEnabled) end
    if _G.AmbitiousMirrorTPDownSetVisual then _G.AmbitiousMirrorTPDownSetVisual(mirrorTPDownEnabled) end
    if _G.AmbitiousTPBatAutoSwingSetVisual then _G.AmbitiousTPBatAutoSwingSetVisual(tpBatAutoSwingEnabled) end
    if _G.AmbitiousTPBatCameraLockSetVisual then _G.AmbitiousTPBatCameraLockSetVisual(_G._AmbitiousGetTPBatCameraLock and _G._AmbitiousGetTPBatCameraLock() or false) end
    _G.AmbitiousTPBatVersion = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.AmbitiousTPBatVersion) or 1, 1, 5)
    if _G._AmbitiousTPBatSetVersionVisual then _G._AmbitiousTPBatSetVersionVisual(_G.AmbitiousTPBatVersion) end
    if _G.AmbitiousTPBatSetVisual then _G.AmbitiousTPBatSetVisual(_G.AmbitiousTPBatOn == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousTPBatOn and _G.AmbitiousStartTPBat then
      _G.AmbitiousStartTPBat()
    elseif _G.AmbitiousStopTPBat then
      _G.AmbitiousStopTPBat()
    end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if selectedAimbotMode == "V2" then
      if _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotStop then _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotStop() end
      if _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2On and _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Start then
        _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Start()
      elseif _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Stop then
        _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Stop()
      end
    else
      if _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Stop then _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Stop() end
      if _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotOn and _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotStart then
        _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotStart()
      elseif _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotStop then
        _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotStop()
      end
    end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.AmbitiousRefreshAimbotVisual() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setBatCounterVisual then setBatCounterVisual(batCounterEnabled) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshBatCounterModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshBatCounterModeSelector() end
    if setMedCounterVisual then setMedCounterVisual(medCounterEnabled) end
    if _SV.setSafeModeVisual then _SV.setSafeModeVisual(antiKickEnabled) end
    if batCounterEnabled then
      if _G.AmbitiousStartBatCounter then _G.AmbitiousStartBatCounter() end
    else
      if _G.AmbitiousStopBatCounter then _G.AmbitiousStopBatCounter() end
    end
    if medCounterEnabled then
      if _G.AmbitiousStartMedCounter then _G.AmbitiousStartMedCounter(LP.Character) end
    else
      if _G.AmbitiousStopMedCounter then _G.AmbitiousStopMedCounter() end
    end
    if _G.AmbitiousSetNoPlayerCollisionVisual then _G.AmbitiousSetNoPlayerCollisionVisual(_G.AmbitiousNoPlayerCollisionEnabled) end
    if _G.AmbitiousNoPlayerCollisionEnabled then
      if enableNoPlayerCollision then enableNoPlayerCollision() end
    else
      if disableNoPlayerCollision then disableNoPlayerCollision() end
    end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setPlayerESPVisual then _SV.setPlayerESPVisual(espEnabled) end
    if espEnabled then if startPlayerESP then startPlayerESP() end; if BoxedESPOptions then BoxedESPOptions.box = true end else if stopPlayerESP then stopPlayerESP() end; if BoxedESPOptions then BoxedESPOptions.box = false end end
    if _SV.setTrackerESPVisual then _SV.setTrackerESPVisual(showTrackerEnabled) end
    if BoxedESPOptions then BoxedESPOptions.tracker = showTrackerEnabled end
    if refreshBoxedESP then refreshBoxedESP() end
    if _SV.setRagdollCountdownVisual then _SV.setRagdollCountdownVisual(ragdollCountdownEnabled) end
    if ragdollCountdownEnabled then
      stopRagdollCountdown()
      if _G.AmbitiousStopEnemyRagdollCountdown then _G.AmbitiousStopEnemyRagdollCountdown() end
      hookRagdollCountdown(LP.Character)
      if _G.AmbitiousStartEnemyRagdollCountdown then _G.AmbitiousStartEnemyRagdollCountdown() end
    else
      stopRagdollCountdown()
      if _G.AmbitiousStopEnemyRagdollCountdown then _G.AmbitiousStopEnemyRagdollCountdown() end
    end
    if _SV.setFPSBoostVisual then _SV.setFPSBoostVisual(fpsBoostEnabled) end
    if fpsBoostEnabled then enableStretchRez() else disableStretchRez() end
    if _SV.setAntiLagVisual then _SV.setAntiLagVisual(antiLagVisualEnabled) end
    if antiLagVisualEnabled then enableAntiLag() else disableAntiLag() end
    if _SV.setVividGraphicsVisual then _SV.setVividGraphicsVisual(vividGraphicsEnabled) end
    if vividGraphicsEnabled then enableVividGraphics() else disableVividGraphics() end
    if _SV.setXRayVisual then _SV.setXRayVisual(xrayEnabled) end
    if setXRay then pcall(setXRay, xrayEnabled) end
    if _SV.setHighPingWarningVisual then _SV.setHighPingWarningVisual(highPingWarningEnabled) end
    if _SV.setKickWarningVisual then _SV.setKickWarningVisual(_G._AmbitiousKickWarn.enabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshUAKVisual then _G.AmbitiousRefreshUAKVisual() end
    if _SV.setFOVVisual then _SV.setFOVVisual(fovEnabled) end
    if fovEnabled then enableCustomFov() else disableCustomFov() end
    if _SV.setNoCamCollisionVisual then _SV.setNoCamCollisionVisual(noCamCollisionEnabled) end
    if noCamCollisionEnabled then enableNoCamCollision() else disableNoCamCollision() end
    if type(applyCustomSky) == "function" then
      applyCustomSky((skyTheme and skyTheme ~= "") and skyTheme or "Off")
    end
    if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = skyTheme or "Off" end
  end)
  pcall(saveAmbitiousConfig)
end
task.defer(function()
  task.wait(0.35)
  if type(applyCustomSky) == "function" then
    pcall(function() applyCustomSky((skyTheme and skyTheme ~= "") and skyTheme or "Off") end)
  end
  if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = skyTheme or "Off" end
end)

customFontVisualEnabled = false
if V then V.customFontEnabled = false end
function enableCustomFont()
  customFontVisualEnabled = false
  if V then V.customFontEnabled = false end
end
function disableCustomFont()
  customFontVisualEnabled = false
  if V then V.customFontEnabled = false end
end
_G.AmbitiousDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates = function()
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setAutoTPVisual then _SV.setAutoTPVisual(autoTPEnabled == true) end
    if autoTPEnabled then startAutoTP() else stopAutoTP() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setInfJumpVisual then setInfJumpVisual(infJumpEnabled == true) end
    if setInfJumpInternal then setInfJumpInternal(infJumpEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshInfJumpModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshInfJumpModeSelector() end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshTPDownMethodSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshTPDownMethodSelector() end   -- >>> TP DOWN METHOD <<<
    if _G.AmbitiousFonts then
      pcall(_G.AmbitiousFonts.applyAll)
      pcall(_G.AmbitiousFonts.hook)
    end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setLookAtVisual then _SV.setLookAtVisual(_G.AmbitiousLookAtEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousLookAtRadiusBox then _G.AmbitiousLookAtRadiusBox.Text = tostring(tonumber(_G.AmbitiousLookAtRadius) or 20) end
    if _G.AmbitiousLookAtEnabled then
      if _G.AmbitiousStartLookAt then _G.AmbitiousStartLookAt() end
    else
      if _G.AmbitiousStopLookAt then _G.AmbitiousStopLookAt() end
    end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setAutoCarrySpeedVisual then setAutoCarrySpeedVisual(autoCarrySpeedEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshAutoCarryModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshAutoCarryModeSelector() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setAntiRagdollVisual then setAntiRagdollVisual(antiRagdollEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshAntiRagdollModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshAntiRagdollModeSelector() end
    if setAntiRagdoll then setAntiRagdoll(antiRagdollEnabled == true) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setAntiDieVisual then _SV.setAntiDieVisual(_G._AmbitiousAntiDieUserWanted == true) end
    setAntiDie(_G._AmbitiousAntiDieUserWanted == true)
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiDieAutoSync then _G.AmbitiousAntiDieAutoSync() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setAutoStealVisual then setAutoStealVisual(autoStealEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousAutoStealSync then _G.AmbitiousAutoStealSync() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setBatCounterVisual then setBatCounterVisual(batCounterEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshBatCounterModeSelector then _G.AmbitiousRefreshBatCounterModeSelector() end
    if batCounterEnabled and _G.AmbitiousStartBatCounter then _G.AmbitiousStartBatCounter() elseif _G.AmbitiousStopBatCounter then _G.AmbitiousStopBatCounter() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if setMedCounterVisual then setMedCounterVisual(medCounterEnabled == true) end
    if medCounterEnabled and _G.AmbitiousStartMedCounter then
      _G.AmbitiousStartMedCounter(LP.Character)
    elseif _G.AmbitiousStopMedCounter then
      _G.AmbitiousStopMedCounter()
    end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _G.AmbitiousSetNoPlayerCollisionVisual then _G.AmbitiousSetNoPlayerCollisionVisual(_G.AmbitiousNoPlayerCollisionEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousNoPlayerCollisionEnabled then enableNoPlayerCollision() else disableNoPlayerCollision() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setUnwalkVisual then _SV.setUnwalkVisual(unwalkEnabled == true) end
    if unwalkEnabled then
      unwalkEnabled = false  -- adesso stopUnwalk passa il suo guard e pulisce il loop
      stopUnwalk()           -- pulisce unwalkLoopConnection residua
      startUnwalk()          -- il guard "if unwalkEnabled then return end" non scatta → avvia il loop
    else
      unwalkEnabled = true   -- forza stopUnwalk a passare il suo guard e fare pulizia
      stopUnwalk()
    end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setSafeModeVisual then _SV.setSafeModeVisual(antiKickEnabled == true) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setPlayerESPVisual then _SV.setPlayerESPVisual(espEnabled == true) end
    if espEnabled then if startPlayerESP then startPlayerESP() end else if stopPlayerESP then stopPlayerESP() end end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setTrackerESPVisual then _SV.setTrackerESPVisual(showTrackerEnabled == true) end
    if BoxedESPOptions then BoxedESPOptions.tracker = showTrackerEnabled == true end
    if refreshBoxedESP then refreshBoxedESP() end
    pcall(function()
      local gui = HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHub")
      if gui then
        for _, obj in ipairs(gui:GetDescendants()) do
          if obj.Name == "ColorPreview" and obj.Parent and obj.Parent.Name == "Tracker Color" then
            obj.BackgroundColor3 = (BoxedESPOptions and BoxedESPOptions.trackerColor) or Color3.fromRGB(230,230,230)
          end
        end
      end
    end)
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setRagdollCountdownVisual then _SV.setRagdollCountdownVisual(ragdollCountdownEnabled == true) end
    if ragdollCountdownEnabled then
      stopRagdollCountdown()
      _G.AmbitiousStopEnemyRagdollCountdown()
      hookRagdollCountdown(LP.Character)
      _G.AmbitiousStartEnemyRagdollCountdown()
    else
      stopRagdollCountdown()
      _G.AmbitiousStopEnemyRagdollCountdown()
    end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setFPSBoostVisual then _SV.setFPSBoostVisual(fpsBoostEnabled == true) end
    if fpsBoostEnabled then enableStretchRez() else disableStretchRez() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setAntiLagVisual then _SV.setAntiLagVisual(antiLagVisualEnabled == true) end
    if antiLagVisualEnabled then enableAntiLag() else disableAntiLag() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setVividGraphicsVisual then _SV.setVividGraphicsVisual(vividGraphicsEnabled == true) end
    if vividGraphicsEnabled then enableVividGraphics() else disableVividGraphics() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setXRayVisual then _SV.setXRayVisual(xrayEnabled == true) end
    if setXRay then setXRay(xrayEnabled) end
    if _SV.setBrainrotXRayVisual then _SV.setBrainrotXRayVisual(_G.AmbitiousBrainrotXRayEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousSetBrainrotXRay then _G.AmbitiousSetBrainrotXRay(_G.AmbitiousBrainrotXRayEnabled == true) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setFOVVisual then _SV.setFOVVisual(fovEnabled == true) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshFovValueBox then _G.AmbitiousRefreshFovValueBox() end   -- >>> FOV VALUE ARROW <<<
    if fovEnabled then enableCustomFov() else disableCustomFov() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setNoCamCollisionVisual then _SV.setNoCamCollisionVisual(noCamCollisionEnabled == true) end
    if noCamCollisionEnabled then enableNoCamCollision() else disableNoCamCollision() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setHeadlessVisual then _SV.setHeadlessVisual(headlessEnabled == true) end
    if headlessEnabled then enableHeadless() else disableHeadless() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _SV.setKorbloxVisual then _SV.setKorbloxVisual(korbloxEnabled == true) end
    if korbloxEnabled then enableKorblox() else disableKorblox() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBat then
      _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBat.setSpam(_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatSpam == true, true)
      _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBat.setVersion(_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatVersion or 1, true)
      _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBat.setEnabled(_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled == true, true)
    end
    if _G._AmbitiousAntiTPBatRefreshUI then _G._AmbitiousAntiTPBatRefreshUI() end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    local on = _G.AmbitiousAntiVoidEnabled == true
    if _SV.setAntiVoidVisual then _SV.setAntiVoidVisual(on) end
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiVoid then _G.AmbitiousAntiVoid.setEnabled(on) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    local on = _G.AmbitiousRainbowToolsEnabled == true
    if _SV.setRainbowToolsVisual then _SV.setRainbowToolsVisual(on) end
    if _G.AmbitiousRainbowTools then _G.AmbitiousRainbowTools.setEnabled(on) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    local on = _G.AmbitiousTransparentToolsEnabled == true
    if _SV.setTransparentToolsVisual then _SV.setTransparentToolsVisual(on) end
    if _G.AmbitiousTransparentTools then _G.AmbitiousTransparentTools.setEnabled(on) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    local on = _G.AmbitiousCustomToolsEnabled == true
    if _SV.setCustomToolsVisual then _SV.setCustomToolsVisual(on) end
    if _G.AmbitiousCustomTools then _G.AmbitiousCustomTools.setEnabled(on) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    local on = _G.AmbitiousCustomSoundsEnabled == true
    if _SV.setCustomSoundsVisual then _SV.setCustomSoundsVisual(on) end
    if _G.AmbitiousCustomSounds then _G.AmbitiousCustomSounds.setEnabled(on) end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if type(applyCustomSky) == "function" then applyCustomSky((skyTheme and skyTheme ~= "") and skyTheme or "Off") end
    if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = skyTheme or "Off" end
  end)
  pcall(function()
    if selectedAnimPackIndex and selectedAnimPackIndex ~= 1 and _G.AmbitiousSetAnimPack then
      _G.AmbitiousSetAnimPack(selectedAnimPackIndex)
    end
    if setAnimPackVisual then setAnimPackVisual(selectedAnimPackIndex or 1) end
    if _G.AmbitiousCustomAnimSetRowVisible then
      _G.AmbitiousCustomAnimSetRowVisible((selectedAnimPackIndex or 1) == 7, false)
    end
  end)
end
task.defer(function()
  task.wait(0.25)
  if _G.AmbitiousDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates then _G.AmbitiousDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates() end
end)
task.delay(1.25, function()
  if _G.AmbitiousDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates then _G.AmbitiousDuelsApplySavedGameplayStates() end
end)
task.delay(3, function()
  if antiLagVisualEnabled and type(applyKTMOptimization) == "function" then pcall(applyKTMOptimization) end

_G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreWanted = _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreWanted or false
_G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil or 0
function ambitiousAnyAimbotActive()
  return (_G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2On == true) or (_G.AmbitiousTPBatOn == true)
end
_G.AmbitiousStopAutoTPForAction = function()
  if autoTPEnabled then
    _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreWanted = true
    _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.35
    stopAutoTP()
    if _SV.setAutoTPVisual then _SV.setAutoTPVisual(false) end
  end
end
function ambitiousTryRestoreAutoTP()
  if not _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreWanted then return end
  if tick() < (_G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil or 0) then return end
  if ambitiousAnyAimbotActive() then return end
  if dropBrainrotActive then return end
  _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreWanted = false
  startAutoTP()
  if _SV.setAutoTPVisual then _SV.setAutoTPVisual(true) end
  saveAmbitiousConfig()
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(ambitiousTryRestoreAutoTP)
_G._oldAmbitiousStopNormalAimbot = _G.AmbitiousStopNormalAimbot
_G.AmbitiousStopNormalAimbot = function(...)
  local r = {_G._oldAmbitiousStopNormalAimbot(...)}
  _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
  task.delay(0.08, ambitiousTryRestoreAutoTP)
  return unpack(r)
end
_G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotStop = _G.AmbitiousStopNormalAimbot
_G._oldAmbitiousStopBatAimbotV2 = _G.AmbitiousStopBatAimbotV2
_G.AmbitiousStopBatAimbotV2 = function(...)
  local r = {_G._oldAmbitiousStopBatAimbotV2(...)}
  _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
  task.delay(0.08, ambitiousTryRestoreAutoTP)
  return unpack(r)
end
_G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Stop = _G.AmbitiousStopBatAimbotV2
do
  local function wrap(name)
    local old = _G[name]
    if type(old) ~= "function" then return end
    _G[name] = function(...)
      local r = {old(...)}
      if _G.AmbitiousAntiDieAutoSync then pcall(_G.AmbitiousAntiDieAutoSync) end
      return unpack(r)
    end
  end
  wrap("AmbitiousStartTPBat")
  wrap("AmbitiousStopTPBat")
  wrap("AmbitiousStartNormalAimbot")
  wrap("AmbitiousStopNormalAimbot")
  wrap("AmbitiousStartBatAimbotV2")
  wrap("AmbitiousStopBatAimbotV2")
  _G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotStart = _G.AmbitiousStartNormalAimbot
  _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Start  = _G.AmbitiousStartBatAimbotV2
  _G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2Stop   = _G.AmbitiousStopBatAimbotV2
end
_G._oldAmbitiousStopTPBat = _G.AmbitiousStopTPBat
_G.AmbitiousStopTPBat = function(...)
  local r = {_G._oldAmbitiousStopTPBat(...)}
  _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
  task.delay(0.08, ambitiousTryRestoreAutoTP)
  return unpack(r)
end
task.spawn(function()
  local wasDropping = false
  while task.wait(0.05) do
    if dropBrainrotActive then
      wasDropping = true
    elseif wasDropping then
      wasDropping = false
      _G.AmbitiousAutoTPRestoreBlockedUntil = tick() + 0.05
      task.delay(0.08, ambitiousTryRestoreAutoTP)
    end
  end
end)
function ambitiousRepairKeybinds()
  for keyId, defaultKey in pairs(DEFAULT_SPEED_KEYBINDS) do
    if speedKeybinds[keyId] == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then
      speedKeybinds[keyId] = defaultKey
    end
  end
  if tpDownKeybind == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then tpDownKeybind = DEFAULT_TP_DOWN_KEYBIND end
  if refreshAllSpeedKeybinds then refreshAllSpeedKeybinds() end
  if refreshTPDownKeybind then refreshTPDownKeybind() end
end
_G._oldSaveAmbitiousConfigStable = saveAmbitiousConfig
saveAmbitiousConfig = function()
  ambitiousRepairKeybinds()
  return _G._oldSaveAmbitiousConfigStable()
end
ambitiousRepairKeybinds()
task.defer(function()
  task.wait(0.2)
  ambitiousRepairKeybinds()
  saveAmbitiousConfig()
end)
task.defer(function()
  task.wait(0.35)
  local TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
  local old = HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubMobileButtons")
  if old then old:Destroy() end
  old = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubMobileButtons")
  if old then old:Destroy() end
  local mobileGui = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
  mobileGui.Name = "AmbitiousHubMobileButtons"
  mobileGui.ResetOnSpawn = false
  mobileGui.IgnoreGuiInset = true
  mobileGui.ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling
  mobileGui.DisplayOrder = 1000
  mobileGui.Parent = HubGui
  _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonRefs = {}
  local mobileButtons = _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonRefs
  function _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonsHidden()
    local g = HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHubMobileButtons")
    if g then g.Enabled = true end
    local hideAll = (_G.AmbitiousHideMobileButtons == true)
    for key, entry in pairs(mobileButtons) do
      local holder = entry and entry.holder
      if holder then
        holder.Visible = not (hideAll and _G.AmbitiousMobileHideIncluded(key))
      end
    end
    if setHideMobileButtonsVisual then pcall(setHideMobileButtonsVisual, hideAll) end
  end
  function _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonSize()
    _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonScale = math.clamp(tonumber(_G.AmbitiousMobileButtonScale) or 0.75, 0.30, 1.35)
    for _, entry in pairs(mobileButtons) do
      local holder = entry and entry.holder
      if holder then
        local sc = holder:FindFirstChild("MobileButtonScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")
        sc.Name = "MobileButtonScale"
        sc.Scale = _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonScale
        sc.Parent = holder
      end
    end
    pcall(function()
      local gui = HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHub") or HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHub") or HubGui:FindFirstChild("AmbitiousHub")
      local root = gui or HubGui
      for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if obj.Name == "Mobile Buttons Size" then
          local valueBox = obj:FindFirstChild("Value")
          if valueBox and valueBox:IsA("TextLabel") then
            valueBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonScale)
          end
        end
      end
    end)
  end
  local function setActive(btn, state)
    if not btn then return end
    local pressed = btn:GetAttribute("AmbitiousMobilePressed") == true
    state = (state == true) or pressed
    local visualState = state and "on" or "off"
    if btn:GetAttribute("AmbitiousMobileVisualState") == visualState then return end
    btn:SetAttribute("AmbitiousMobileVisualState", visualState)
    local holder = btn.Parent
    local glow = holder and holder:FindFirstChild("Glow")
    local st = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
    TS:Create(btn, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
      BackgroundColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(238,238,238) or Color3.fromRGB(8,8,8),
      TextColor3 = state and Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0) or Color3.fromRGB(225,225,225),
      TextTransparency = 0,
    }):Play()
    if st then
      TS:Create(st, TweenInfo.new(0.18), {
        Color = state and Color3.fromRGB(190,190,190) or Color3.fromRGB(85,85,85),
        Thickness = 1,
        Transparency = state and 0 or 0.4,
      }):Play()
    end
    if glow then
      glow.Visible = false
      glow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
      local gs = glow:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
      if gs then gs.Transparency = 1 end
    end
  end
  local function pulse(btn)
    if not btn then return end
    btn:SetAttribute("AmbitiousMobilePressed", true)
    setActive(btn, true)
    task.delay(0.18, function()
      if btn and btn.Parent then
        btn:SetAttribute("AmbitiousMobilePressed", false)
        setActive(btn, false)
      end
    end)
  end
local function makeButton(key, label, pos, onPress)
    local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
    holder.Name = "MBH_" .. key
    holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 78, 0, 58)
    local savedPos = _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonPositions and _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonPositions[key]
    if savedPos and type(savedPos) == "table" then
        holder.Position = tableToUDim2(savedPos, pos)
    else
        holder.Position = pos
    end
    holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
    holder.ZIndex = 1000
    holder.Active = true
    holder.Parent = mobileGui
    local btn = Instance.new("TextButton", holder)
    btn.Name = "MB_" .. key
    btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
    btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
    btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
    btn.BorderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    btn.BorderMode = Enum.BorderMode.Inset
    btn.Text = label
    btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    btn.TextSize = 10
    btn.TextWrapped = true
    btn.AutoButtonColor = false
    btn.ZIndex = 1002
    btn.Active = true
    Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 10)
    local txtGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", btn)
    txtGrad.Name = "_AmbitiousShimmerGradient"
    txtGrad.Rotation = 0
    txtGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
    txtGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(220, 100, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, Color3.fromRGB(120, 60, 220)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(40, 20, 150)),
    })
    local bgGrad = Instance.new("UIGradient", btn)
    bgGrad.Name = "BgGradient"
    bgGrad.Rotation = 0
    bgGrad.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0)
    bgGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(220, 100, 255)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, Color3.fromRGB(120, 60, 220)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(40, 20, 150)),
    })
    bgGrad.Enabled = false  -- disabilitato di default
    local glow = Instance.new("Frame", holder)
    glow.Name = "Glow"
    glow.Size = UDim2.new(1, 4, 1, 4)
    glow.Position = UDim2.new(0, -2, 0, -2)
    glow.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 50, 255)
    glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.9
    glow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    glow.ZIndex = 1000
    Instance.new("UICorner", glow).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 13)
    local function updateButton(state)
        if state then
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)  -- base per gradiente
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)              -- TESTO NERO
            txtGrad.Enabled = false                               -- disabilita gradiente testo
            bgGrad.Enabled = true                                 -- abilita gradiente sfondo
            glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        else
            btn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
            btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)       -- base per gradiente
            txtGrad.Enabled = true                                -- abilita gradiente testo
            bgGrad.Enabled = false                                -- disabilita gradiente sfondo
            glow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.9
        end
    end
    local function getLogicalState()
        if key == "autoPlay" then
            return _G.AmbitiousAutoPlay and _G.AmbitiousAutoPlay.active() or false
        elseif key == "aimbot" then
            return (_G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2On == true)
        elseif key == "tpBat" then
            return _G.AmbitiousTPBatMobileActive and _G.AmbitiousTPBatMobileActive() or false
        elseif key == "antiTPBat" then
            return _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled == true
        elseif key == "carry" then
            return currentSpeedMode == "Carry"
        elseif key == "laggerNormal" then
            return currentSpeedMode == "Lagger"
        elseif key == "laggerCarry" then
            return currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry"
        else
            return false
        end
    end
    local pressing = false
    local dragging = false
    local pressPos = nil
    local holderStart = nil
    btn.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            pressing = true
            dragging = false
            pressPos = i.Position
            holderStart = holder.Position
            updateButton(true)
        end
    end)
    btn.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if pressing and not dragging then
                pcall(onPress)
            end
            if dragging then
                if _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonPositions then
                    _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonPositions[key] = udim2ToTable(holder.Position)
                end
                pcall(saveAmbitiousConfig)
            end
            pressing = false
            dragging = false
            task.delay(0.02, function()
                if btn and btn.Parent then
                    updateButton(getLogicalState())
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
    UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if _G.AmbitiousGuiLocked == true or not pressing then return end
        if i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement and i.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Touch then return end
        local delta = i.Position - pressPos
        if not dragging and (math.abs(delta.X) > 6 or math.abs(delta.Y) > 6) then
            dragging = true
        end
        if dragging then
            holder.Position = UDim2.new(
                holderStart.X.Scale,
                holderStart.X.Offset + delta.X,
                holderStart.Y.Scale,
                holderStart.Y.Offset + delta.Y
            )
        end
    end)
    mobileButtons[key] = {
        holder = holder,
        btn = btn,
        setActive = function(state) updateButton(state) end
    }
    if _G.AmbitiousMobileHideIncluded then
        holder.Visible = not ((_G.AmbitiousHideMobileButtons == true)
                              and _G.AmbitiousMobileHideIncluded(key))
    end
    updateButton(getLogicalState())
    return btn
end
  local x1, x2, x3 = -218, -154, -90
  local y1, y2, y3, y4 = -150, -102, -54, -6
local defaults = {
    drop         = UDim2.new(1, x2, 0.5, y1),
    autoPlay     = UDim2.new(1, x3, 0.5, y1),
    tpBat   = UDim2.new(1, x1, 0.5, y1),
    aimbot       = UDim2.new(1, x2, 0.5, y2),
    instantReset = UDim2.new(1, x1, 0.5, y2),
    antiTPBat    = UDim2.new(1, x1, 0.5, y3),
    float        = UDim2.new(1, x3, 0.5, y2),
    tp           = UDim2.new(1, x2, 0.5, y3),
    carry        = UDim2.new(1, x3, 0.5, y3),
    laggerNormal = UDim2.new(1, x2, 0.5, y4),
    laggerCarry  = UDim2.new(1, x3, 0.5, y4),
}
  function _G.AmbitiousResetMobileButtons()
    _G.AmbitiousMobileButtonScale = 0.75
    _G.AmbitiousHideMobileButtons = false
    _G.AmbitiousMobileHideList = {}
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshMobileHideList then pcall(_G.AmbitiousRefreshMobileHideList) end
    for key, defaultPos in pairs(defaults) do
      local entry = mobileButtons[key]
      local holder = entry and entry.holder
      if holder then
        holder.Position = defaultPos
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(0, 78, 0, 58)
        local btn = entry.btn
        if btn then
          btn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
          btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
          btn:SetAttribute("AmbitiousMobilePressed", false)
          btn:SetAttribute("AmbitiousMobileVisualState", nil)
        end
      end
    end
    if _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonSize then _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonSize() end
    if _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonsHidden then _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonsHidden() end
    if showActionNotification then pcall(function() showActionNotification("MOBILE BUTTONS RESET") end) end
    saveAmbitiousConfig()
  end
  makeButton("drop", "DROP\nBR", defaults.drop, function(btn)
    if selectedDropMode == "Stand Still" then
      if runDropStandStill then runDropStandStill() end
    else
      if runDropBrainrot then runDropBrainrot() elseif runDrop then runDrop() end
    end
    pulse(btn)
  end)
  makeButton("autoPlay", "AUTO\nPLAY", defaults.autoPlay, function(btn)
    if _G.AmbitiousAutoPlayToggle then _G.AmbitiousAutoPlayToggle() end
    task.delay(0.03, function()
      setActive(btn, _G.AmbitiousAutoPlay and _G.AmbitiousAutoPlay.active() or false)
    end)
  end)
  makeButton("tpBat", "TP\nBAT", defaults.tpBat, function(btn)
    if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked and _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked() then
      if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeForceStop then _G.AmbitiousSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
      return
    end
    if _G.AmbitiousToggleTPBatManual then
      _G.AmbitiousToggleTPBatManual()
    elseif _G.AmbitiousToggleTPBat then
      _G.AmbitiousToggleTPBat()
    end
    task.delay(0.03, function()
      setActive(btn, _G.AmbitiousTPBatMobileActive and _G.AmbitiousTPBatMobileActive() or false)
    end)
  end)
  local function _getAimbotMobileLabel()
    if selectedAimbotMode == "V2" then return "BAT\nAIMBOT\nV2" end
    return "BAT\nAIMBOT\nV1"
  end
  makeButton("aimbot", _getAimbotMobileLabel(), defaults.aimbot, function(btn)
    if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked and _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked() then
      if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeForceStop then _G.AmbitiousSafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
      return
    end
    if _G.AmbitiousToggleSelectedAimbot then _G.AmbitiousToggleSelectedAimbot() end
    if _G.AmbitiousRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.AmbitiousRefreshAimbotVisual() end
    task.delay(0.03, function()
      setActive(btn, (_G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2On == true))
    end)
  end)
  makeButton("instantReset", "INSTANT\nRESET", defaults.instantReset, function(btn)
    if _G.AmbitiousInstantReset then _G.AmbitiousInstantReset() end
    pulse(btn)
  end)
  makeButton("antiTPBat", "ANTI\nTP BAT", defaults.antiTPBat, function(btn)
    if _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBat then
      _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBat.toggle()
    elseif _G.AmbitiousToggleAntiTPBat then
      _G.AmbitiousToggleAntiTPBat()
    end
    task.delay(0.03, function()
      setActive(btn, _G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled == true)
    end)
  end)
  makeButton("float", "FLOAT", defaults.float, function(btn)
    if _G.AmbitiousFloatToggle then _G.AmbitiousFloatToggle() end
    task.delay(0.03, function()
      setActive(btn, _G.AmbitiousFloatOn == true)
    end)
  end)
  makeButton("tp", "TP\nDOWN", defaults.tp, function(btn)
    if runTPFloor then runTPFloor() end
    pulse(btn)
  end)
  makeButton("carry", "CARRY\nSPEED", defaults.carry, function(btn)
    if setSpeedMode then setSpeedMode(currentSpeedMode == "Carry" and "Normal" or "Carry") end
    task.delay(0.03, function()
      if mobileButtons.carry then mobileButtons.carry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Carry") end
      if mobileButtons.laggerNormal then mobileButtons.laggerNormal.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger") end
      if mobileButtons.laggerCarry then mobileButtons.laggerCarry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") end
    end)
  end)
  makeButton("laggerNormal", "LAGGER\nNORMAL", defaults.laggerNormal, function(btn)
    if setSpeedMode then setSpeedMode(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger" and "Normal" or "Lagger") end
    task.delay(0.03, function()
      if mobileButtons.carry then mobileButtons.carry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Carry") end
      if mobileButtons.laggerNormal then mobileButtons.laggerNormal.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger") end
      if mobileButtons.laggerCarry then mobileButtons.laggerCarry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") end
    end)
  end)
  makeButton("laggerCarry", "LAGGER\nCARRY", defaults.laggerCarry, function(btn)
    if setSpeedMode then setSpeedMode(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry" and "Normal" or "Lagger Carry") end
    task.delay(0.03, function()
      if mobileButtons.carry then mobileButtons.carry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Carry") end
      if mobileButtons.laggerNormal then mobileButtons.laggerNormal.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger") end
      if mobileButtons.laggerCarry then mobileButtons.laggerCarry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") end
    end)
  end)
  _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonSize()
  _G.AmbitiousApplyMobileButtonsHidden()
  function _G.AmbitiousRefreshMobileButtonStates()
    if mobileButtons.autoPlay then
      mobileButtons.autoPlay.setActive(_G.AmbitiousAutoPlay and _G.AmbitiousAutoPlay.active() or false)
    end
    if mobileButtons.aimbot then
      mobileButtons.aimbot.setActive((_G.AmbitiousNormalAimbotOn == true) or (_G.AmbitiousBatAimbotV2On == true))
      local aimbotMobileBtn = mobileButtons.aimbot.btn
      if aimbotMobileBtn then
        aimbotMobileBtn.Text = (selectedAimbotMode == "V2") and "BAT\nAIMBOT\nV2" or "BAT\nAIMBOT\nV1"
      end
      if _G._AmbHideOptLabel_aimbot then pcall(_G._AmbHideOptLabel_aimbot) end
    end
    if mobileButtons.float then
      mobileButtons.float.setActive(_G.AmbitiousFloatOn == true)
    end
    if mobileButtons.tpBat then
      mobileButtons.tpBat.setActive(_G.AmbitiousTPBatMobileActive and _G.AmbitiousTPBatMobileActive() or false)
    end
    if mobileButtons.antiTPBat then
      mobileButtons.antiTPBat.setActive(_G.AmbitiousAntiTPBatEnabled == true)
    end
    if mobileButtons.carry then mobileButtons.carry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Carry") end
    if mobileButtons.laggerNormal then mobileButtons.laggerNormal.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger") end
    if mobileButtons.laggerCarry then mobileButtons.laggerCarry.setActive(currentSpeedMode == "Lagger Carry") end
  end
end)
local _oldLoadAmbitiousConfig = loadAmbitiousConfig
loadAmbitiousConfig = function()
    _oldLoadAmbitiousConfig()
    if _G.AmbitiousSetStarbornFont then
        pcall(_G.AmbitiousSetStarbornFont, _G.AmbitiousStarbornFontEnabled == true, true)
        if _G._AmbitiousStarbornFontSetVisual then
            pcall(_G._AmbitiousStarbornFontSetVisual, _G.AmbitiousStarbornFontEnabled == true)
        end
    end
    if _G.StretchRez and _G.StretchRez.apply then
        local idx = stretchCurrentIndex or 1
        _G.StretchRez.apply(idx)
    end
end
